60. 【2611.25539】COVERAGE-TRACE: Source-Side Counterfactual Tracing for Detecting Omissions in LLM Summaries

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25539

作者：Ali Karkehabadi,Jamshid Hassanpour,Rafael E. Banchs

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：missing information leaves, span to inspect, excluding a decision, difficult to verify, verify because missing

备注： Accepted at LCFM @ NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Omissions are difficult to verify because missing information leaves no output span to inspect: a summary may be factually supported while still excluding a decision, qualification, quantity, or condition needed to preserve the source's meaning. We introduce COVERAGE-TRACE, a source-side counterfactual framework that evaluates omission as dependence on a designated atomic source fact. It replaces only the target fact with type-matched alternatives, holds the candidate summary fixed, and measures the resulting change in its teacher-forced, length-normalized likelihood. A summary covering the target should respond more strongly than one omitting it, while aggregation across multiple counterfactuals reduces sensitivity to replacement choice. We evaluate COVERAGE-TRACE on 80 matched covered--omitted QMSum pairs spanning several fact types and four open-weight models. Multi-CF achieves 0.764--0.825 AUROC, compared with 0.640 for the strongest evaluated text-only baseline. On Falcon-7B, targeted interventions achieve 0.779 AUROC, whereas shuffling counterfactual assignments reduces performance to 0.533; target deletion is effective while unrelated deletion is not. However, adjustment for meaning-preserving paraphrases reduces AUROC to 0.591--0.624. COVERAGE-TRACE therefore provides a practical, complementary signal of target-specific source--summary dependence, but not a wording-invariant semantic coverage probability. These results establish source-side intervention as a promising approach to omission detection while identifying semantic invariance as the central remaining challenge.

Abstract:Recent work reports strong pragmatic performance by large language models (LLMs) in English, yet whether this performance generalises across languages and evaluation settings remains unclear. In this paper, we first introduce PILAPS, a bilingual English/Mandarin Chinese benchmark covering eight pragmatic phenomena across purpose-written and dialogue-derived items, with both culturally adapted and independently sourced data. Together, these findings demonstrate that strong pragmatic performance in one evaluation setting does not necessarily generalise across languages or conversational contexts.

67. 【2611.25433】RoCoKV: Accelerating LLM Inference Serving through Uncertainty and Redundancy Aware KV Cache Compression

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25433

作者：Payman Behnam,Elaheh Sobhani,Saad Amin,Yahya Hassan,Daniyal Khan,Biswadeep Chakraborty,Tanish Patwa,Souvik Kundu,Alexey Tumanov

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：generated reasoning traces, input prompt, reasoning, cache compression, large reasoning models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:KV cache compression is critical for large reasoning models, where most of the cost comes from long generated reasoning traces rather than the input prompt. These traces often contain redundant and low-utility tokens due to looping, self-verification, and corrections. However, such tokens may still receive high attention scores, making it difficult to remove them without harming the reasoning process. We propose RoCoKV, a KV cache compression method designed for large reasoning models. RoCoKV introduces two key components. First, uncertainty-guided adaptive pruning adjusts the compression strength based on the model's confidence, allocating more memory during uncertain phases and applying stronger pruning when the model is confident. Second, redundancy-aware token selection reduces the impact of tokens that are highly similar to recent reasoning content, encouraging the model to retain more informative and diverse tokens. Beyond the algorithm, we co-design RoCoKV with the serving stack: we develop fused three-phase CUDA decode kernels and integrate them into vLLM as a drop-in plugin that preserves its paged KV layout and CUDA graph capture, turning algorithmic savings into real end-to-end speedups. Extensive experiments across three reasoning benchmarks and two reasoning models demonstrate that RoCoKV consistently improves reasoning accuracy under constrained KV cache budgets, achieving higher accuracy compared to state-of-the-art KV cache compression methods. For DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B in vLLM, RoCoKV achieves a 1.27X speedup and 38% higher throughput on AIME25, the most challenging evaluated reasoning benchmark, and a 1.43X speedup and 45% higher throughput on the long synthetic benchmark, compared with Full-KV.

68. 【2611.25429】Evolve Where It Matters: Coverage-Guided Self-Evolution of Agent Skills

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25429

作者：Boyin Tan,Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma,Chun Jason Xue,Xiaowei Huang,Youcheng Sun

关键词：package procedural knowledge, skills package procedural, Agent skills package, package procedural, procedural knowledge

备注： 15 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agent skills package procedural knowledge as natural-language instructions, and a skill helps only when its instructions are correct and properly followed by the agent. Self-evolving agents revise their own skills from the runs they make, but existing methods judge each run as a whole, by its outcome or by where it went wrong, and cannot tell whether a failure comes from a wrong instruction or from one the agent did not follow. Instruction-level coverage was introduced to test skills and labels each instruction of a run as follow, not-follow, or not covered, which separates these causes. We leverage it for skill self-evolution with SkillPlumber, a framework in which an agent evolves its own skills where coverage shows they fall short. A persistent root cause analysis agent turns each label into a revision question and checks each revision against the next run, while a maintainer distills investigations across tasks into a shared strategy library. Across three benchmarks and three executors, SkillPlumber solves 64.7% to 70.5% of the tasks, 25.6 to 30.4 percentage points higher than the initial skill and 8.7 to 14.0 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline, and removing coverage reduces recovered failures by 20.8% on SkillsBench with GPT-5.5.

Abstract:Argumentation frameworks (AFs) provide explicit, formal mechanisms for representing and resolving conflicts and thus have been incorporated into large language model (LLM) decision-making. The benefits vary and depend on how arguments and their relations are constructed, evaluated, and incorporated into the decision process. {We abstract a workflow consisting of four stages based on existing argumentation-augmented methods} and investigate their design choices over tasks of debate-based decision-making and retrieval-based fact verification. 71. 【2611.25357】Pro$^2$ Agent: Scientific Problem Probing for Autonomous AI RD

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25357

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language model (LLM) agents can now autonomously conduct AI research by writing code and running experiments. They typically do so by iteratively optimizing a codebase, while ignoring the problem structure behind the task. This formulation is effective on simpler tasks, but on difficult problems it often gets stuck in the wrong solution region because the agent never explicitly represents its understanding of the problem. We introduce Pro$^2$ Agent, which shifts the primary state of autonomous AI research from a codebase state to a Problem State: an explicit structure of what problems prevent success and which solutions are likely to address them. Pro$^2$ Agent initializes the Problem State through comprehensive analysis of available resources and constraints. Through controlled hypothesis testing, the agent updates the importance of a suspected problem or the benefit brought by a candidate solution. On the complete MLE-Bench, Pro$^2$ Agent matches the best reported medal rate (70.7%) under the standard 24-hour budget and reaches 76.0% with a 48-hour budget, the highest medal rate reported on the complete benchmark. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25314

Abstract:Language models are increasingly used to support human judgement in complex reasoning tasks, yet their predictions often remain difficult to interpret and validate. Explainability techniques provide insight into model decision-making, helping expose errors, failure modes, and potential bias so users can decide whether to rely on model outputs. Explanations are simplified approximations of a language model's internal workings. The goal is to obtain explanations that reflect the underlying computation as closely as possible (faithfulness), are understandable to users (plausibility), and help them achieve their intended goals (utility). Evaluation protocols are essential for assessing these dimensions. This thesis studies highlight explanations, natural language explanations, and chain-of-thought reasoning. This thesis aims to improve people's understanding of models' decision-making processes in complex reasoning tasks. First, it proposes two evaluation frameworks for assessing highlight explanations across multiple properties and explanation types, together with a meta-evaluation framework for examining the reliability of existing faithfulness metrics for chain-of-thought explanations. Second, it proposes two techniques that generate natural language explanations designed to be faithful to the model's prediction process while remaining concise, coherent, and helpful for understanding model decisions. Finally, a study of human-AI interaction explores the roles of evidence and explanations in helping users validate model verdicts in a fact-checking setting.

73. 【2611.25289】LLMs Are Already Confidence-Aware Zero-Shot Classifiers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25289

作者：Owen Cook,Xingyi Song

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Zero-shot large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as text classifiers, but their labels are only useful when we know to what extent we can trust them. We compare black-box methods (verbalised confidence and sampling) with white-box methods that read token probabilities (P(True), and multiple logit-scoring methods using constrained generation). We evaluate eleven open-weight LLMs (1B-32B parameters) on five classification datasets with 3 to 151 labels, measuring classification performance (accuracy, F1-macro), discrimination (AUROC), and calibration (ECE and Brier score). The choice of confidence method rarely impacts accuracy significantly, but logit-based scoring methods lead to much better discrimination (AUROC 0.74-0.77) than black-box methods (AUROC 0.57-0.66). Replacing labels with single-token identifiers preserves discrimination but costs 3-6 accuracy points on average. We also introduce decision-only scoring, which only scores positions where labels diverge in the prefix tree (trie). It reduces decode steps by 88-96% on the three largest label sets and performs comparably to full-label scoring. The best open-weight model-method configurations (Gemma 4 31B and Qwen 3.5 9B) are competitive with the commercial Jev 1.13.0 classifier.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575), Swiss Reformer and Antistes of Zurich, documents the political and religious debates of the Reformation, including extensive appeals to the Church Fathers as authoritative sources. Detecting such references computationally remains largely unexplored. We investigate dense and sparse retrieval for patristic reference detection, and metadata-informed topic modelling as a proxy for the topics and ideas discussed. A Latin encoder fine-tuned on biblical reuse performs best among the bi-encoders (82%, Recall@20 on explicit and 16% on implicit references), while BM25 outperforms dense approaches on explicit citations (94%, R@20). However, dominant topic weights barely exceed a uniform distribution, making topics unusable to inform retrieval. Together, these results present a first approach to retrieving patristic references and modelling fine-grained topics in Bullinger's correspondence.

76. 【2611.25214】Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25214

作者：Fangxu Yu,Tao Feng,Xuying Ning,Haolin Yang,Jiaxuan You,Tianyi Zhou

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：identifying promising directions, discovery requires revisiting, requires revisiting, identifying promising, Scientific discovery requires

备注： Technical Report

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Scientific discovery requires revisiting the hypotheses in light of new evidence and identifying promising directions beyond those already explored. However, research agents often make claims that are not supported by their observations, explore a narrow hypothesis space, and retain beliefs despite recognizing conflicting evidence. These failures motivate the search for an explicit epistemic state that captures both beliefs about existing hypotheses and directions for further exploration. To this end, we propose Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass), a plug-and-play scaffold that maintains this state through an epistemic tree linking hypotheses, evidence, beliefs, and revisions. As an investigation progresses, EpiCompass reassesses the hypotheses explored against accumulated evidence and uses unresolved conflicts and unexplored directions to guide hypothesis generation. It selects candidates by anticipated epistemic gain, accounting for both refinement of existing hypotheses and expansion of the hypothesis space, and verifies the final conclusion against the accumulated evidence. Experimental results across three scientific discovery benchmarks and three LLM backbones demonstrate that EpiCompass consistently outperforms baselines, with further analyses showing reductions in errors of unsupported claims and failures to revise beliefs.

78. 【2611.25191】Blind Spots Travel, Knowledge Stays Home: What RL Inherits from an LLM Judge

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25191

作者：Liang Yue,Eric A. Vance

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：LLM judges serve, LLM judges, answer, answer key, answer key exists

备注： 31 pages, 6 figures, 27 tables. Code and data: [this https URL](https://github.com/liyu2195/blind-spots-travel)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LLM judges serve as rewards where no answer key exists, in the hope that their knowledge passes to the trained model. In 814 RL runs that teach 7-8B models to answer closed-book questions or abstain, we ask what passes. What a grader knows about single answers stays home. A wrong answer that a judge rewards is memorised for that one question. A model trained with the answer key, or with a far stronger judge, chooses which new questions to answer no better than the untrained model does by sampling 32 answers and abstaining where they disagree; the grader's knowledge adds about one corrected answer per hundred questions. What a grader believes about a kind of question travels. A judge that rejects eleven of the twelve correct training answers about capitals of former states teaches the model to refuse 98% of such capitals on new questions, a loss that accuracy and abstention rates do not reveal. Changing only those twelve verdicts by hand moves the refusal from 5% to 90%, even for a grader that accepts as many answers as the answer key; at that leniency, two of four judges already cost 38-42% of these capitals. The capitals are not forgotten: with "I don't know" blocked, the model answers 94% of them correctly. The grader therefore needs no knowledge of its own: rewarding the model for giving the answer that most of its own untrained samples agree on, with no labels and no judge, earns the answer key's reward and keeps the category.

79. 【2611.25167】Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25167

作者：Mark Lovett,Denis Lim,Soroush Vosoughi

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)

关键词：experts, expert, allocation, game, Abstract

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game's kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135

作者：Rudransh Agnihotri,Manasvi Kapoor

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

备注： 24 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

81. 【2611.25123】Forget Freely, Grep Later: Lossless, Index-Free Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25123

作者：Ziyan Yang,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao

备注： 17 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables. Ziyan Yang and Taiwei Shi contributed equally

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long-horizon LLM agents often exceed their context windows, forcing them to discard information that may be needed later. Existing context management strategies rely on summarization, masking, or structured memory, requiring decisions about what to preserve or how to retrieve it before future needs are known. However, these methods can introduce lossy compression, potentially discarding details needed for future tasks. To address this limitation, we introduce GrepMem, a simple, lossless, and index-free memory mechanism built on two components: an append-only plain-text log of the agent's complete history and a regular-expression search tool for on-demand recall. We train Qwen3.5-9B with reinforcement learning under five context management strategies and evaluate on multi-question research sessions, long-stream recall, and agent-trajectory question answering. GrepMem achieves 95-97% accuracy on ContextBench, versus at most 21% for lossy baselines, and outperforms SUPO's summarization by 8.8 percentage points on BCP-Multi. GrepMem scales effectively to increasingly long input streams while remaining on the accuracy-token-cost Pareto frontier across all five evaluation tasks. Our results show that a plain-text log and grep provide a simple but competitive approach to long-horizon agent memory, without requiring complex structured memory infrastructure.

82. 【2611.25109】CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109

作者：Shwetanshu Singh,Mark Keavney,Mathieu Duteil,Meriem Mehri,Scott Blain

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：form emotional attachments, anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional, attachment, parasocial

备注： 25 pages, 7 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) write fluent financial analyses but miscompute ratios, recall stale prices, and route questions to the wrong tool with high confidence. We ask which component should do each job in a financial question-answering assistant, and study it with FinVeriQA, an open-source multi-agent research prototype over SEC filings and live market data: a compact typed-decision model (Laya) routes each question and screens out advice requests, Python computes every number, retrieval and tools fetch evidence, Mistral-7B writes a cited answer, a code validator checks that cited IDs exist and that numbers match computed notes, and a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server exposes the pipeline. In live experiments, Laya routed better than Mistral-7B (0.85 vs. 0.52; overall 0.86 vs. 0.68, McNemar p = 0.0003); a 12B Mistral decided no better, and DeepSeek-R1-8B decided better (0.79 vs. 0.68, p = 0.0192) but about 9.9 times more slowly. Mistral-7B computed 29% of 100 financial calculations correctly against 100% for Python, a live quote cut price error from 210% to 0.00%, and hybrid retrieval reached 76% Hit@5 on 50 filing questions. Yet in a live case study, both agent answers passed validation while describing AMD sample data under NVIDIA's and Alphabet's names. A controlled upper-bound study on the FinRank benchmark explains why: when one figure in the shared evidence is corrupted, self-consistency stays confident (agreement 0.55 to 0.59 while accuracy falls from 0.50 to 0.00), and a shared-evidence check (the kind our validator performs) catches 35% of the errors, whereas a check against the original passage catches 77% ($p = 5.96 \times 10^{-8}$); for DeepSeek-R1-8B, 68% vs. 90%. Verification must be independent of the evidence the writer saw.

85. 【2611.25003】A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25003

作者：Manuel Prada-Corral,Yahya Emara,Timothy J. O'Donnell,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Formal Languages and Automata Theory (cs.FL); Machine Learning (stat.ML)

关键词：high-quality individual samples, repeated samples clustering, produce high-quality individual, Language models, individual samples

备注： NeurIPS 2026 paper

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler's output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality--diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision--recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.

86. 【2611.24994】What the model knows but does not say: monitoring medical LLMs through verbalized thoughts

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24994

作者：Frederik Hauke,Jan Clusmann,Hanna Kreutzer,Christiane Kuhl,Tianyu Han,Felix Barajas Ordonez,Sven Nebelung,Jakob Nikolas Kather,Daniel Truhn

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Efforts to keep clinical language models safe have concentrated on what enters and leaves the model, but a manipulated model can return an answer that looks entirely ordinary. Here we show that the model's own internal state, rendered in plain language, exposes manipulations the output conceals. Using a natural-language autoencoder, we translate the activations of a multimodal LLM (Gemma-3-27B) into text and let an input-blind judge read this verbalized internal state (the thoughts channel) and the model's answer separately. Across two clinical attack objectives (omitting a pathology, covertly recommending a drug), two input modalities, and two delivery mechanisms, the thoughts channel caught the injection significantly more often than the answer in all eight injected conditions (p0.05), while both channels held near-zero false-positive rates on clean inputs; the omission result carried over to Llama-3.3-70B. The thoughts channel also registered unsafe clinical requests as harmful while the model was still reading them, before it decided whether to refuse. None of this requires retraining: an off-the-shelf verbalizer and a general-purpose judge are enough.

Abstract:Populations vary along cultural dimensions such as individualism, collectivism, and honor. 88. 【2611.24977】CANDID: Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution in Local Computer-Use Agents

Abstract:We present CANDID (Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution), an execution-control framework designed to enhance the reliability of local computer-use agents (CUAs) in resource-constrained settings. 90. 【2611.24971】he ROI of Small Examples: Optimizing In-Context Learning Under Token Budgets

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24971

作者：Anxo Perez,Jorge Gabín,Javier Parapar

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：In-context learning, updating model parameters, improves large language, improves large, time without updating

备注： Accepted for EACL 27 Main Conference

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In-context learning (ICL) improves large language model performance at inference time without updating model parameters. However, these gains are not free: in practical deployments, longer prompts directly increase inference cost and latency. Under a fixed token budget, prompt construction therefore becomes a resource-allocation problem. While prior work has often focused on how many examples to include in ICL, we study the complementary question of whether the available budget is better spent on a few long demonstrations or on a larger number of shorter ones. We study this aspect in question answering (QA), by organizing candidate demonstrations into length-based bins and constructing prompts from examples within the same length range under fixed budgets. Experiments on two QA benchmarks (\textit{Beat the AI} and \textit{Natural Questions}), using six instruction-tuned LLMs, show that shorter demonstrations are generally the most effective use of prompt space. This effect is especially strong on \textit{Beat the AI}, and remains visible on NQ, although gains are smaller and saturate earlier in the long-context setting. An additional natural language inference experiment on ANLI R3 finds that one of the two shorter bins performs best for five of six models, although longer demonstrations remain competitive. Overall, our results highlight demonstration granularity as an important factor in prompt design.

92. 【2611.24969】Global Importance Estimation for KV Cache Eviction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24969

作者：Seojin Kim,Noseong Park

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：cache remains, remains a central, central bottleneck, Abstract, applying Personalized PageRank

备注： The 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Memory consumption of the key-value (KV) cache remains a central bottleneck for long-context LLM inference. We introduce PRKV, a training-free KV eviction method that estimates multi-hop global token importance by applying Personalized PageRank over attention-induced transition graphs. This enables importance propagation beyond recency or instantaneous attention, without modifying the model. 93. 【2611.24963】Physics of Language Models: Part 4.2, Canon Layers at Scale where Synthetic Pretraining Resonates in Reality

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24963

作者：Zeyuan Allen-Zhu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：architectural differences, Gated DeltaNet, gated, gated linear attention, linear

备注： Results, recipes, and code have been public since December 9, 2025 at [this https URL](https://github.com/facebookresearch/PhysicsLM4)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Do standard benchmarks see the architectural differences that matter? We study it with 64 from-scratch pretraining runs of Transformers and three linear-model families at 1.3-8B parameters on 1-2T Nemotron-CC tokens, at a cost of roughly 100k USD per 8B run. In academic-scale pretraining (1.3B parameters, 100B tokens), architectural differences can all but disappear when the models are carefully tuned. Instead, at 1-2T tokens they become measurable: Canon layers, which add local token mixing, consistently improve Transformers, saving about 30% of pretraining tokens for the same MMLU accuracy at 8B. We also introduce GLA5 and GDN2, which improve gated linear attention and Gated DeltaNet with low-rank output gates. Surprisingly, with Canon, the simplest linear model, gated linear attention, matches Gated DeltaNet and outperforms Mamba2 even at 8B/1T. Therefore, the extra machinery of Gated DeltaNet and Mamba2 may do little more than mix neighboring tokens. Even at 8B/1T, however, high scores on standard benchmarks can hide weaknesses in basic in-context skills. In particular, linear models that match or beat Transformers on these benchmarks cannot, in fact, reliably retrieve information from a short 500-token context. Moreover, no model, Transformer or linear, reliably beats random guessing on arguably the simplest two-hop question, stated in a few short sentences, even at this scale. These findings $\textbf{challenge traditional architecture research}$ based primarily on standard benchmark scores. In contrast, small synthetic playgrounds, which predicted the architectural differences above at 100M parameters, can reveal what these benchmarks miss. For practitioners, one lesson is to include in-context reasoning examples in pretraining data from an early stage, rather than reserve them for later mid-training, let alone post-training.

94. 【2611.24960】ETHER: Training-Free, Elastic, ToC-Anchored Hierarchy Extraction and Recovery in Design-Driven Long Documents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24960

作者：Yu Zou,Yan Chen,Jialei Zeng,Xiaorui Zhou,Aixi Zhong,Qi Zhou,Haoran You,Ke Jiang,Changyu Hu

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Converting complex documents into structured knowledge constitutes the critical infrastructure urgently required for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in downstream research. However, blind to the Table of Contents (ToC) as a macro-structure, existing bottom-up paradigms rely on localized visual cues, inducing heuristic fragility in high-entropy layouts where visual prominence decouples from logical depth; meanwhile, direct LLM extraction lacks spatial grounding, triggering hallucinations in long contexts. To address this, we introduce TETHER, a training-free framework that elevates the ToC from discarded noise to a vital structural prior, enabling robust adaptation across design-driven long documents. TETHER operates via a two-stage neuro-symbolic mechanism: first, it constructs a document skeleton from the raw ToC using a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) empowered by spatio-semantic constraints; second, it employs an anchor-based alignment strategy to tether content blocks to this skeleton, effectively resolving boundary ambiguities. Crucially, ablations confirm the framework's architectural irreducibility, while achieving 90% token reduction and drastic speedups over end-to-end baselines, establishing a compelling equilibrium between structural precision and deployment scalability.

95. 【2611.24959】ICARE: cross-domain text classification with incremental class-aware representation and distillation learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24959

作者：Son Duong,Truong X. Tran,Hai-Anh Tran

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The rise of powerful, fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) necessitates robust natural language inference (NLI) systems. These NLI systems ideally train across various tasks, encompassing diverse concepts like sentiment analysis, news classification, and coreference resolution. However, this continual, multi-tasking approach can lead to catastrophic forgetting, where previously learned tasks are lost, especially in domains lacking specific labels. Rehearsing past examples with labels can further exacerbate this issue by causing overfitting, hindering the system's ability to classify new categories. To tackle these challenges, this research proposes ICARE, a novel framework with two key components: an instance selection module and a knowledge distillation method. The former is based on representation learning, which meticulously selects training examples to present to the NLI system. The latter, knowledge distillation with feature and logit-level transfer, facilitates knowledge transfer between tasks. This leverages pruning techniques and optimized objective functions to transfer both essential feature-level and decision-making (logit-level) knowledge, mitigating catastrophic forgetting while adapting to new patterns. Extensive evaluations on benchmark text classification datasets have demonstrated ICARE's effectiveness. The framework achieves a significant improvement in target accuracy and reduction in the ``forgetting metric''. These results solidify ICARE's ability to preserve and leverage learned knowledge across diverse tasks, ultimately enhancing the adaptability and performance of NLI systems. The source code is available at this https URL

96. 【2611.24943】LLM Complexity: Towards a Computational Complexity View of Large Language Model Inference

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24943

作者：Yu-Hung Wu,Shao-Xiang Yuan,Chan-Hung Yu,Zih-Ching Chen,Kuan-Hao Huang,Shao-Hua Sun

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Large Language Models, computational systems executing, Large Language, Computational complexity theory, complexity theory offers

备注： 10 pages, 20 with Appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Computational complexity theory offers a principled framework for characterizing the efficiency of computational problems, and Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly be viewed as computational systems executing a structured process of input, inference-time compute, and output. In this context, the number of generated tokens emerges as a natural and directly observable proxy for inference-time compute. However, we show that the tempting analogy between LLM token length and classical time complexity does not hold. We disprove the hypothesis that inference-time token usage aligns monotonically with classical time complexity, demonstrating a fundamental divergence grounded in how LLMs actually allocate computation during inference. To this end, we introduce LLM Complexity, a new metric designed to quantify problem complexity from the perspective of LLMs by characterizing the area bounded above by the accuracy-token curve. We find that diverse LLMs induce highly consistent task rankings under LLM Complexity, indicating that the metric captures stable notions of difficulty across models. We leverage this property to construct an easy-to-hard curriculum for supervised fine-tuning, which improves accuracy while reducing token consumption. Finally, we demonstrate that LLM Complexity generalizes to real-world tasks, producing more stable cross-model correlations than human-annotated difficulty labels.

1. 【2611.26609】Variants of Beam Search for DiskANN

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26609

作者：Viraj Agrawal,Kirankumar Shiragur

类目：Data Structures and Algorithms (cs.DS); Databases (cs.DB); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Graph-based data structures, alpha, large-scale approximate nearest-neighbor, Graph-based data, NSG constitute

备注： 22 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Graph-based data structures such as DiskANN, HNSW, and NSG constitute the state of the art for large-scale approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search. Indyk and Xu introduced the notion of $\alpha$-reachable graphs, which include graphs built by DiskANN's slow-preprocessing construction, and proved that greedy search achieves an approximation factor of $\tfrac{\alpha+1}{\alpha-1}$. Independently, Al-Jazzazi et al. proposed adaptive beam search (ABS) with a relaxation parameter $\gamma 0$, showing a $\tfrac{2}{\gamma}$ approximation on navigable graphs ($\alpha \approx 1$), where greedy search admits no guarantees. In this work, we unify and generalize these results by proving that ABS with parameter $\gamma$ on $\alpha$-reachable graphs achieves an approximation of $\tfrac{\alpha+1}{\alpha(\gamma+1)-1}$ for any metric. Moreover, on sorted $\alpha$-reachable graphs (introduced by Gollapudi et al.) under the $\ell_2$ metric, we obtain the tighter bound $\max\bigl(\tfrac{\alpha}{\alpha(\gamma+1)-1}, \tfrac{\alpha^{2}+1}{\alpha^{2}(\gamma+1)-1}\bigr)$. Beyond these theoretical results, we design a beam predictor using features inspired by fine-grained analysis of ABS, trained via a constrained objective that minimizes mean distance computations subject to a target recall. On three 100-million-point benchmarks, using graphs built by DiskANN's practical construction, beam predictor lowers mean query latency on SSD-resident indices at matched recall by up to $29.6\%$ relative to classical beam search (CBS). Relative to ABS, it is faster in $11$ of $15$ settings, by up to $6.5\%$, and at most $1.4\%$ slower in the other $4$; at $95\%$ recall, its advantage over ABS grows from $0.8$--$4.2\%$ for $100$ neighbors to $4.6$--$5.6\%$ for $500$ neighbors. In memory, it needs $1.3$--$23.7\%$ fewer distance computations than CBS and $0.0$--$3.9\%$ fewer than ABS.

2. 【2611.26531】Cost-Aware Agentic Feature Optimization System for Fleet-Scale Ranking Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26531

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large Web platforms operate large fleets of ranking models, each selecting features from a shared pool of tens of thousands. We present an end-to-end agentic system that optimizes features across the fleet under resource constraints shared across models and those set per model, such as feature pipeline cost and serving throughput (QPS), respectively. In this system, LLM agents propose hypotheses, and deterministic tools execute experiments and verify them. For each model, an LLM generates and shortlists cross features from a combinatorial candidate space, and all features are then ranked by several importance methods. Feature blending mixes these rankings into a single selection, using Pareto fronts across tasks for multi-task models. An LLM Researcher agent then tunes the feature selection in 10--25 asynchronous trials under resource caps, and a trimming step keeps the pipeline cost within budget. Across production models, the system improves normalized entropy by 0.31% on average within each model's budget, and the trimming step cuts pipeline cost by 2.3% with no regression beyond measurement noise. The optimized models were launched, and in online A/B tests they improved a primary online metric by 0.20%.

3. 【2611.26521】he Window Lever: Longer Observation Windows Outweigh the Observed Effects of Data, Capacity, and Architecture in Customer Behavior Forecasting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26521

作者：Tara Sowrirajan,Yanqiao Wang

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：long-horizon customer-behavior forecasting, observation window outweighed, longer observation window, customer-behavior forecasting, newer architectures

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In our experiments on long-horizon customer-behavior forecasting, a longer observation window outweighed the observed effects of more data, more capacity and newer architectures. The window is the weeks of history a model sees before the week it forecasts from. Going from an 8- to a 16-week window adds a raw +0.070 to 0.107 log-space $R^2$ in every family at 1M. Matched test cohorts, which score the same customer-weeks at every window, show this raw comparison understates the gain in every family they cover. The direction holds on five public datasets, four spanning over a year (ridge and our transformer on all five). On our internal data, longer windows admit up to 10% more and colder training customers, which matching does not fix. Our design does not separate window length from calendar coverage, because every test anchor (the week a forecast is made from) falls in one eight-week band. On training-period anchors, the weekly ridge probe's gain persists outside the holiday season, keeping about 72% of it. We therefore report a robust positive association with longer windows and do not identify the magnitude of a calendar-independent history effect. Of four internally registered predictions, the only fully blind one failed. An activity-mixture model predicted +0.014 from 24 to 32 weeks; the weekly-aggregate probe returned -0.0004. For the sequence families, the other three levers are about an order of magnitude smaller, bounded and unresolved. Their observed effects are at most 0.006 between the nominal 1M and 10M tiers and 0.0052 among five sequence families at 16 weeks with clocks as built, both single-seed on the full test split, and 0.006 for capacity on three-seed means. These are observed intervals, not equivalence bounds. Our claim is the order-of-magnitude contrast between the window and those bounds. A team building on this class of data should start by lengthening the window.

Abstract:Personalized content recommendation on large marketplace websites must reconcile heterogeneous candidate content (e.g., customer stories, product pages, industry pages, and learning content) with sparse, fast-changing user behavior, placing increasing demands on the retrieval stage to represent user interest well, not just the downstream ranking stage. We present LSER (LLM Session Embedding Retrieval), a production content recommendation system for this http URL that derives session- and history-level embeddings from a pretrained large language model (LLM) and adds them as auxiliary user-side features in the retrieval-stage user tower alongside existing learned sequence encoders. To keep user-side embeddings fresh under real-time behavior, we build a nearline serving path that asynchronously refreshes sequential and LLM-derived user vectors for our main content recommendation server. We validate the incremental value of the LLM features through an ablation study across our full multi-content-type catalog. Our results indicate that integrating LLM-derived embeddings as additional retrieval signals, together with production-grade nearline serving, is technically feasible and can improve model performances.

7. 【2611.25845】LINEAGE: A Benchmark for Empirical Lineage Retrieval in AI Scientific Literature

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25845

作者：Pangjing Wu,Xu Yuan,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Current research agents, Current research, Lineage, research agents, moving beyond academic

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Current research agents are moving beyond academic search toward literature synthesis and experiment planning. These workflows increasingly rely on literature retrieval systems, which serve as standalone systems or as components of research agents. However, existing scientific literature retrieval benchmarks mainly evaluate these methods using topical relevance or citation links as supervision, which cannot capture which prior methods are used for empirical comparison. To address this gap, we introduce Lineage, a benchmark for empirical lineage retrieval in AI scientific literature. An empirical lineage relation links a prior paper to a source paper that explicitly uses its method as an empirical comparison baseline. These relations form lineage graphs whose multi-hop neighborhoods provide retrieval ground truth for structural evaluation. To construct these graphs at scale, we develop Lineage-Constructor, which converts heterogeneous paper records and full-paper empirical baseline evidence into calibrated lineage graphs. Built from 121,048 papers across 17 top AI venues from 2018 to 2025, Lineage contains 3,950 graph components, 67,502 paper nodes, 164,948 lineage edges, and 80,000 multi-facet retrieval queries. Experiments with 19 representative retrieval systems show that empirical lineage retrieval remains challenging. Performance drops sharply with lineage distance, while structural metrics reveal that isolated retrieval hits often fail to recover closely connected empirical comparison neighborhoods.

8. 【2611.25738】CACO: Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization for Continuous Generative Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25738

作者：Pan Zhang,Nan Zhuang,Yongkang Zhang,Kun Xú,Zhongtao Jiang,Zhiwei Wang,Kun Xǔ,Youhuan Li

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Continuous generative retrievers, parallel approximate nearest-neighbor, fixed retrieval budgets, Continuous generative, diffusion sample multiple

备注： 21 pages, 16 figures, 12 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Continuous generative retrievers powered by flow matching and diffusion sample multiple queries in item embedding space for parallel approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search under fixed retrieval budgets. However, optimizing distribution diversity or target-level interest separation ignores set-level retrieval geometry: sampled queries collapse onto overlapping ANN neighborhoods, while discrete top-K lookup and single-target supervision block end-to-end coverage optimization. We introduce CACO (Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization), a lightweight self-supervised post-training framework that coordinates queries from frozen flow-matching and diffusion retrievers toward complementary item coverage with negligible additional inference latency. CACO uses empirical retrieval frequencies across sampled reference queries as a relevance proxy, formulating a label-free coverage objective that improves recall across all 30 backbone-dataset settings in offline greedy selection experiments. To avoid the inference overhead of generating a large query pool, CACO employs a coordinated query adjustment network that combines inter-query bidirectional attention with gated relative directions, finally updating queries by bounded tangent-space projection and spherical retraction. During training, CACO applies tangential antithetic perturbations and measures each query's marginal gain via counterfactual single-query replacement, yielding an unbiased gradient estimator of smoothed set coverage across non-differentiable ANN search. On eight public benchmarks and an in-house 24-partition streaming industrial dataset, CACO improves Recall@100 by up to 23.7% and Recall@400 by up to 23.9% over the strongest baseline, cuts inter-query overlap by 53.3%-64.9%, and adds only 0.5%-3.0% inference latency.

9. 【2611.25733】Can LLMs Resolve Entity Conflicts in Domain Specific NLU Pipelines?

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Resolving entity conflicts is a critical step in Pipeline-based NLU architectures, especially when multiple extractors provide overlapping or conflicting outputs. Traditional rule-based entity conflict resolution methods often lack flexibility and are unscalable. In this work, we aim to study if the broad knowledge of LLMs can be leveraged to resolve entity conflicts in domain specific NLU pipelines. We present an experimental comparison between a traditional rule-based conflict resolution method, and two LLM-based approaches: a multi-shot prompting method using the Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model and a LoRA fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25705

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long-term memory is essential for personalized agentic systems that interact with users over long time periods, yet current architectures only capture what happened while leaving the reasoning of why it happened to be derived later by underlying LLMs.%, and silo knowledge within individual users. These events can, however, convey not only what a user experienced but also how that experience shaped their behavioral patterns. We introduce STARK, a dual-graph memory architecture which treats these events not just as nodes but also as edges between behavioral insights. STARK maintains an Episodic Graph which records interactions as temporally, causally, and semantically linked episodes; and an Insight Graph which represents LLM-extracted behavioral signals scored by how many events support them, connected by the very events that led to them. Evaluations show that STARK improves by 1.48 judge score on LongMemEval information extraction and 0.93 on LoCoMo open domain questions over state of the art. 11. 【2611.25691】Exploring Semantic Table Search in Data Lakes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25691

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

备注： 29 pages, 13 figures, GitHub repository at [this https URL](https://github.com/dkw-aau/Thetis-extended)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Discovery of relevant datasets within data lakes remains a key challenge. Union and join search are effective techniques for discovering data when augmenting an existing table. In exploratory search, instead, keyword search is commonly adopted to find a relatively small set of highly relevant tables matching some search terms. In this context, semantic search is a new technique that exploits the presence of named entities in exemplar tuples used as queries and in target tables. Hence, keyword search and entity search are able to find a diverse set of tables. In this work, we propose \texttt{Thetis++}, which combines keyword and semantic table search to discover relevant tables that combine the semantic signal with the presence of exact keyword matches. Incorporating multiple paradigms and sources of information allow us to combine the best of multiple worlds. Therefore, we study many alternative techniques for combining rankings and evaluate these for the table search task in data lakes. We explore both unsupervised and supervised techniques, including TOPSIS. Finally, alternative to Thetis++ combining keyword and semantic relevance signals, we study a variant of learning-to-rank for the table search task in data lakes. Our evaluation highlights both the limitations of many standard approaches along with some promising directions offered by learned approaches. Furthermore, while indexes for keyword search are quite efficient, here we provide insights into how to improve the scalability of prefiltering techniques for semantic search, comparing LSH indexes with Hierarchical Navigable Small Worlds. Our experiments show that HNSW drastically improves the runtime of semantic table search by 4.7X on average whilst retaining the high-quality tables. Together, our findings plot the line for effective and efficient data discovery techniques for semantic data lakes.

12. 【2611.25659】DNA: Unlocking Depth Scaling in CTR Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25659

作者：Caiyuan Li,Wenjie Wu,Yuheng Huang,Xiao Zhou,Silu Zhou,Ke Chen,Zinan Zhao,Xinrui Zeng,Hanmo Xu,Chaoqun Song,Hua Zong,Zongyuan Wu,Hongbin Zhou,Huimin Yi,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Long user behavior, Long user, click-through rate, strongest signals, signals for click-through

备注： 10 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long user behavior sequences are among the strongest signals for click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Cross-attention makes these sequences practical for industrial ranking by keeping compute linear in sequence length. Yet the gains from stacking cross-attention layers taper off: on Taobao search, growing a cross-attention stack from 16 to 28 layers adds only 0.02 percentage points (pt) of AUC. We argue that the key-value side is the bottleneck. Each layer projects the same behavior sequence into keys and values, so adding depth gives the model more reads of a fixed sequence. We propose DNA (Dual-stream Nexus Attention), which lets the sequence itself evolve with depth. At each layer, the request representation (the search query, user features and scene) first reads the sequence. Each behavior token then attends to the updated request representation and uses what it reads to update itself, so the next layer reads a sequence shaped by the current request. The update runs once per request, all candidates share the resulting sequence, and both the reads and the update keep linear complexity in sequence length. At comparable forward FLOPs, DNA improves AUC over cross-attention stacks by 0.08 to 0.26 pt and keeps improving in the depth range where those stacks level off. DNA-4 is deployed in Taobao search. In an online A/B test against the production model, it improves CTR by 0.8% and GMV by 0.5%.

14. 【2611.25606】Generative News Recommendation Reproduces Popularity: A Memorization-Based Analysis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25606

作者：Shotaro Ishihara

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：directly generating item, generating item titles, ranking existing items, Text-based generative recommenders, existing items

备注： The 11th International Workshop on Application of Big Data for Computational Social Science (ABCSS2026 @ IEEE BigData 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-based generative recommenders can go beyond ranking existing items by directly generating item titles or other textual representations. In the news domain, this raises a societal concern that models may repeatedly surface already popular articles and thereby narrow the diversity of information to which users are exposed. We investigate training-data memorization as one mechanism behind this concentration. Using approximately 20 million browsing sessions from a Japanese news service, we continually trained an instruction-tuned Llama 3 8B model to generate the next article title in a session. The generated-title distribution was more concentrated and less diverse than the distribution of titles observed in the browsing logs. Frequently repeated target titles exhibited greater verbatim and approximate memorization, and more strongly memorized titles were generated in response to a wider range of prompts. These findings support a pathway in which popularity in behavioral logs becomes repeated training text, is preferentially memorized, and is subsequently reproduced by a generative recommender. An exploratory target-level deduplication condition reduced both memorization and output concentration, while substantially reducing the training-set size and recommendation accuracy. Our results suggest that popularity bias in generative recommendation may arise not only from user preferences or ranking objectives, but also from the model's tendency to reproduce frequently encountered strings, with important implications for information diversity on news platforms.

16. 【2611.25527】Learning Generative Ranking Policies for Whole-Page Optimization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25527

作者：Chen Qiu,Zhigang Hua,Qi Xu,Jizhe Zhang,Sha Hua,Ren Mao,Luke Simon

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：compete for attention, attention and complement, simply the individually, page, pages

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A recommendation page is consumed as a whole: the items on it compete for attention and complement one another, so the best page is not simply the individually best items placed side by side. We study whole-page optimization, which selects and orders the items on a page jointly to maximize the score of the page. The number of possible pages grows combinatorially, so good pages must be generated rather than enumerated. Some generative re-ranking methods use the same page-level training signal for every choice in a sampled page. Others imitate target pages built before training, which remain largely fixed as the generator improves. We address both limitations with one local page-level objective, which repeatedly moves the current generator toward pages with higher score. This objective splits exactly into one training target per position, and each target compares candidates at the same partial page by the scores of the complete pages they lead to. Every position therefore learns to choose the item that is better for the whole page, rather than the item that looks best on its own. We call this procedure whole-page policy improvement. On two public datasets, KuaiRand-1K and RL4RS-SeqSlate, which cover multi-objective item selection and constrained ordering, and on an industrial dataset of logged pages, our method outperforms the evaluated generative re-ranking baselines. On a synthetic benchmark with a known optimal page, it also comes closest to the optimum, showing that its improvements come from better whole-page decisions.

18. 【2611.25256】LQ-Rec: Late Quantization for Sequential Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25256

作者：Satoshi Koide,Yueqi Wang,Julian McAuley

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：sequential recommendation compresses, generate next-item codes, Generative retrieval, user history, sequential recommendation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative retrieval (GR) for sequential recommendation compresses each item into a short semantic ID and trains an autoregressive decoder to generate next-item codes from a user history. While appealing for cold-start handling and storage, au- toregressive GR suffers a structural scaling limit: typically, longer semantic IDs reduce quantization error in principle but degrade ranking quality and quadratic memory usage against semantic ID length. We propose Late Quantization (LQ), a non-autoregressive paradigm that confines discretization to the output (item) side of the pipeline. The encoder and scoring rule operate in continuous space, while items are stored and matched as semantic IDs produced by an end-to-end-trained residual quantizer. This architectural shift permits an additive error decomposition and a pairwise ordering theorem that jointly bound the quantization- induced ranking drop, a structural guarantee absent in prior semantic-ID recommenders. Across four public benchmarks, the resulting recommender, LQ-Rec, delivers three capabilities simultaneously: ranking quality improves monotonically with the semantic-ID length and outperforms existing baselines; a single trained model supports anytime compression, tuning ranking quality against inference cost without retraining; and LQ-Rec shows superior generalization on a memorization/generalization partition.

19. 【2611.25247】Retrieving Patristic References and Modelling Topics in Bullinger's Reformation Correspondence

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25247

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Digital Libraries (cs.DL)

备注： Accepted for the Seventh Conference on Computational Humanities Research 2027. To be published in the Anthology of Computers and the Humanities

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575), Swiss Reformer and Antistes of Zurich, documents the political and religious debates of the Reformation, including extensive appeals to the Church Fathers as authoritative sources. Detecting such references computationally remains largely unexplored. We investigate dense and sparse retrieval for patristic reference detection, and metadata-informed topic modelling as a proxy for the topics and ideas discussed. Prior work on Latin intertextuality has fine-tuned dense retrievers for their respective target domain, and we test how well these models transfer to Bullinger's correspondence. We compare lexical (BM25), dense (bi-encoders), and hybrid methods on explicit and implicit references. A Latin encoder fine-tuned on biblical reuse performs best among the bi-encoders (82%, Recall@20 on explicit and 16% on implicit references), while BM25 outperforms dense approaches on explicit citations (94%, R@20). Because BM25 and dense retrieval recover disjoint sets of implicit references, fusing their rankings raises implicit R@20 to 22%, close to cross-encoder reranking of the same candidates (26%) but without its inference cost. For topic modelling, we extend FASTopic with a seed-guided loss function and editorial abstract embeddings. Measurable coherence gains over the baseline remain minimal, but qualitative and temporal analysis shows that some topics track documented historical events. However, dominant topic weights barely exceed a uniform distribution, making topics unusable to inform retrieval. A pilot application of LLM-based concept induction (LLooM) further points towards a more human-interpretable alternative. Together, these results present a first approach to retrieving patristic references and modelling fine-grained topics in Bullinger's correspondence.

20. 【2611.25229】LSP-RAG: Latent Scoring Perspectives for Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25229

作者：Haochen Liu,Guanghui Min,Song Wang,Yada Zhu,Chen Chen,Jundong Li

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in open-domain question answering, but often struggle with factual accuracy and multi-hop reasoning due to the incompleteness of the training corpora. A promising solution is Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG), which supplements LLMs with structured knowledge retrieved from external knowledge graphs (KGs). However, existing KG-RAG methods either rely on large-scale language models (e.g., ChatGPT) to guide the retrieval process, which leads to high computational costs, or suffer from limited retrieval quality when using lightweight language models, particularly under multi-hop scenarios. We propose LSP-RAG (Latent Scoring Perspectives for Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Knowledge Graphs), a novel KG-RAG framework that enhances hop-wise KG knowledge retrieval through multi-perspective prompted scoring. Each latent scoring perspective is guided by a combination of two types of soft prompts: a perspective-specific soft prompt induces a distinct prompt-conditioned scoring function over candidate triplets, while a contextual soft prompt evolves with each reasoning hop by encoding the query and previously explored KG triplets, enabling the model to preserve consistency and relevance across multi-hop retrieval. Rather than assuming manually defined semantic roles, these latent perspectives are adaptively aggregated to capture heterogeneous relevance signals during retrieval. This design allows LSP-RAG to perform accurate and robust retrieval using lightweight language models. LSP-RAG achieves superior performance across multiple KG-based Question Answering benchmarks compared to existing retrieval systems, including those that rely on much larger language models, demonstrating its effectiveness under limited computational budgets.

计算机视觉

1. 【2611.26629】Posterior Matching Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26629

作者：Hidir Yesiltepe,Julius Berner,Arash Vahdat,Pinar Yanardag

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diffusion distillation accelerates, Diffusion distillation, pretrained teacher iterative, iterative sampling process, compressing a pretrained

备注： Project Page: [this https URL](https://pmd-distillation.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion distillation accelerates image and video generation by compressing a pretrained teacher's iterative sampling process into a few network evaluations. Distribution-based methods achieve this by matching the student's output distribution to the teacher's. This matching is often driven by reverse-KL objectives, whose mode-seeking bias favors dominant teacher modes at the expense of diversity across samples. We introduce Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD), a new member of the distribution-based distillation family that combines mode-covering posterior sampling with any-step generation. At zero population loss, PMD recovers the teacher's full posterior of clean images at each trained noise level. Its stochastic student supports any-step sampling budgets within the trained timestep grid without further training. To learn these posteriors, PMD matches exponentially reweighted averages of student samples conditioned on a fixed noisy state to corresponding teacher posterior means. Under Gaussian corruption, exponentially tilted posterior mean is a single teacher evaluation at a shifted input. We prove that, matching tilted means over an open neighborhood uniquely identifies the full posterior. PMD thus supervises the entire conditional distribution through a family of mean constraints, without any auxiliary student-score network. Experiments on SDXL and FLUX.2 [klein] 4B demonstrate that PMD achieves the best FID among 4-step methods on both models, while maintaining competitive or leading performance across prompt-alignment and perceptual-quality metrics.

2. 【2611.26627】RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26627

作者：Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Alec Helbling,Atakan Kara,Duen Horng Chau,Pinar Yanardag

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Reinforcement learning, post-training substantially improves, visual quality, diffusion transformers, substantially improves

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://rl-is-steering.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training substantially improves the visual quality, text rendering, and compositional accuracy of diffusion transformers. Because capabilities such as rendering requested text or composing multiple objects are inherently prompt-dependent, a natural hypothesis is that improving them requires learning complex, input-dependent transformations. To isolate how much of these gains an input-independent shift explains, we probe each RL-tuned model with a deliberately simple parameterization: a fixed set of steering vectors added to the block outputs of the frozen base model. On SD3.5-M, these vectors hold 65x fewer parameters than the RL-tuned LoRA (0.29M against 18.8M). We fit them to match the velocity predictions of public RL-tuned checkpoints, with no reward in the objective. Although the vectors are identical for every prompt, token, and sampling seed, they recover 90.0-96.3% of the reward gains of six RL-tuned image models, and 89.8% and 111.9% on two RL-tuned video models. Initialized at zero and trained with online RL, the vectors alone exceed the reward gains of weight-based RL on text rendering (OCR) and human preference (PickScore) on SD3.5-M. A constant shift thus suffices for most of the gain of policy-gradient RL post-training: without new input-dependent transformations, it steers the pretrained generator toward what it can already compute.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26624

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent AI agents can generate playable games, but these games are not necessarily engaging. Starting from an initial game, PlayCraft alternates between playing and crafting. In the playing stage, a player agent watches the game screen, uses the controls, and pursues the game objective over repeated episodes while retaining memory. We evaluate games in fresh runs with player agents across three intelligence settings, measuring learning gain and learning slope. Compared with the initial games, PlayCraft yields larger learning gains and steeper learning slopes in most games. Abstract:Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:Native 3D generation has largely been built around latent diffusion, with recent advances concentrating on stronger variational autoencoders (VAE) and increasingly elaborate latent representations, from vecset and triplanes to structured sparse voxels. Despite their architectural differences, these methods all share the same premise: geometry is first compressed into a latent space and only then modeled by the generator. We take a different route and propose Direct3D-V3, a latent-free diffusion transformer (DiT) framework that discards the VAE altogether and operates in the native voxel domain, directly modeling raw SDF voxel grids during both training and sampling. To make this direct voxel formulation geometrically reliable, we introduce an implicit manifold regularization over neighboring voxels to suppress non-watertight surfaces, and convert predicted SDF grids into meshes via Marching Cubes so that rendered normal losses can supervise the model directly from its predicted SDF. To improve alignment between the conditioning image and generated geometry, Direct3D-V3 also predicts the camera parameters of the conditioning image as an auxiliary task, encouraging the DiT to learn the mapping from image observations to 3D shape. This latent-free design removes the reconstruction bottleneck, brings training under an objective in the native voxel domain, and yields meshes with substantially richer local detail. The predicted camera and native SDF voxel output further make Direct3D-V3 extensible to generative reconstruction, where diffusion is interleaved with normal-guided refinement to improve alignment with the observed view while the generative prior completes unseen regions. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26493

作者：Aoxiang Fan,Maria Pilligua,Corentin Dumery,Oliver Heinimann,Pascal Fua

关键词：view patches densely, hoc attention designs, patches densely, designs that relate, attention

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward models have been very successful in 3D reconstruction with ad hoc attention designs that relate all view patches densely. However, the cost of this dense global attention grows quadratically with the number of views, preventing the model from applying to larger-scale reconstruction settings. In this paper, we argue that most dense attention computations are unnecessary from a classical structure-from-motion perspective: 3D structures stem from geometrically matching regions in 2D. We bring this principle to learning-based feed-forward reconstruction with our Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction (SGAR) method. In SGAR, each patch token attends to only a small set of patch tokens from other views, chosen by a lightweight learned key selection module trained to predict cross-view 3D proximity of tokens. Thus, dense global attention is replaced by much more efficient geometric-similarity-guided sparse attention. SGAR is a general method that can be applied to different feed-forward models by fine-tuning. In our experiments, we show that SGAR is the fastest among both state-of-the-art dense and sparse methods and the most accurate sparse one. It runs up to 12-times faster than dense attention at 1,000 views with stable pose accuracy where existing sparse methods fail, and shows the best performance in reconstructing more complete geometries on the same time budget.

18. 【2611.26478】SHUSH: Emission-Aware Reward Design for Proactive Video Assistants

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26478

作者：Shawn Liang,Soamar Homsi,Gourav Datta

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Proactive video assistants, Proactive video, Proactive, reward, video assistants

备注： under review at ICASSP

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Proactive video assistants must decide not only what to say, but also when to speak. Existing reinforcement-learning objectives can reward timely, correct responses without accounting for response volume, thereby encouraging unnecessary or repeated emissions. We identify this failure mode in MMDuet2, whose proactive area-under-the-curve (PAUC) reward promotes timely responses but does not price the number of emissions. We show analytically that this makes additional responses weakly penalized or effectively free, systematically encouraging over-emission. We then propose an emission-aware F1 reward for interval-valued annotations, using exclusive span--emission matching to price false emissions and missed spans. Under MMDuet2's training recipe, our policy on the visual subset of OmniPro replies 2.5x less often than MMDuet2 while raising timing F1 from 0.259 to 0.396 and content-aware F1 from 0.103 to 0.154; a more conservative variant cuts replies by 82.5% and raises precision from 0.161 to 0.418. On ProactiveVideoQA, the same reward adds replies where the baseline is nearly silent, and PAUC ranks a heavily over-emitting policy second despite near-last F1, because it is insensitive to response count. Our results indicate that proactive assistants should be trained and evaluated with objectives that price emissions.
