点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in image restoration have greatly improved global image fidelity, producing visually plausible results across most regions of an image. Nevertheless, even high-quality restorations often exhibit localized failures, including artifacts in semantically important regions such as faces and text, structural inconsistencies, and texture mismatches. Although these errors typically affect only a small portion of the image, they often dominate the overall perceptual quality of the result. In this work, we propose Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR) to address the "last-mile" problem in image restoration. Rather than applying one uniform generative prior over the whole image, we condition a single-pass restoration model on both where local failures are likely to occur and what corrective guidance each region requires. This region-bound conditioning enables fine-grained and controllable restoration that improves semantic accuracy and stylistic consistency in critical regions while maintaining faithful, high-quality restoration elsewhere. Our method complements existing restoration pipelines by shifting the emphasis from global fidelity to local perceptual acceptability. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach resolves common failure cases and improves perceptual quality, bridging the gap between technical accuracy and practical usability.

20. 【2611.26444】Mind2Act World: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Embodied Mind, Physical Action, and Mind-Action Composition in Robotic Manipulation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26444

作者：Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Fanding Huang,Junchao Lin,Weichen Zhang,Ziyi Xiong,Yang Li,Zhengwei Xie,Zhimin Wang,Zhen Liu,Jiazhen Huang,Yuzheng Zhang,Yuche Liu,Hongyi Yang,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang,Hakan Aktas,Adam Wu,Duo Wu,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Huanan Liu,Weijue Bu,Chenxin Li,Xinyuan Wang,Tianyi Bai,Yinan Mao,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Heng Yu,Ehsan Adeli,Minchen Li,Yong-Lu Li,Cengiz Öztireli,Huazhe Xu,Chao Yu,Jing Zhang,Shanghang Zhang,Weitao Zhou,Wenwu Zhu,Cheng Chi,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Embodied intelligence aims, perform complex physical, robots perform complex, history-based cognitive reasoning, Embodied intelligence

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Embodied intelligence aims to let robots perform complex physical tasks with human-like adaptability and reliability, which requires three complementary capabilities: history-based cognitive reasoning, precise and timely physical execution, and the composition of reasoning and execution through environmental feedback. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate reasoning and execution within the same tasks or assess them separately without their composition, so they reveal little about whether a failure comes from reasoning, execution, or their composition. We introduce Mind2Act World, a diagnostic benchmark of 30 simulated manipulation tasks from five real-world application domains. Each task has three difficulty levels and two scene settings (Clean and Random), yielding 180 configurations. The tasks form three views: Mind tests decisions based on past observations, Act tests precise and timely execution, and Mind2Act combines both in closed-loop tasks where action outcomes guide later decisions. Comparing the three views shows whether a system lacks a capability or fails to compose capabilities it already has. A diagnosis harness further attributes the failed episodes of three representative systems to Mind, Act, or their composition and summarizes each system in a profile card. Our evaluation of multimodal agents, vision-language-action models (VLAs), and world-action models (WAMs) shows that competence in a single view does not carry over to Mind2Act tasks. Even the strongest agent succeeds in 52.7% of Mind and 39.0% of Act episodes but in only 12.0% of Mind2Act episodes. The strongest agent retains task state but struggles with sustained contact, whereas end-to-end policies execute practiced motions but retain little task state. These findings point to composing reasoning and execution through environmental feedback as the main open problem.

21. 【2611.26438】Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438

作者：Zixuan Liu,Hashem Haghbayan,Zizhan Zheng,Siavash Khajavi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：updating model parameters, models enable efficient, model parameters, efficient test-time adaptation, updating model

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA's predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2--11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

22. 【2611.26437】Asuka-FM: A System-Architectural Co-Design Framework for Efficient Text-to-Image Pretraining on Consumer Hardware

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26437

作者：Víctor Rodrigo González,Pablo M. Olmos,Ignacio Peis,Alejandro Lancho

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：parameter Diffusion Transformers, Diffusion Transformers, massive compute clusters, models predominantly rely, multi-billion parameter Diffusion

备注： 40 pages, 19 figures, 17 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://deeplearningcafe.github.io/projects/asuka-fm) ; Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/deeplearningcafe/asuka-fm)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Frontier text-to-image (T2I) foundation models predominantly rely on multi-billion parameter Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) pretrained across massive compute clusters, creating an accessibility barrier for academic laboratories. When training DiTs from scratch on single consumer GPUs (e.g., an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 24\,GB VRAM), practitioners encounter three primary bottlenecks: quadratic attention sequence costs, Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation graph breaks induced by variable aspect-ratio bucketing, and an autoencoder throughput wall where frozen Variational Autoencoder (VAE) operations dominate step latency. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Asuka-FM}, an end-to-end system and architectural framework demonstrating that competitive dual-stream DiTs ($\sim$206M parameters) can be pretrained from scratch on a single consumer GPU in $\sim$160 GPU hours for under \$55. Evaluated on open-domain benchmarks without benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Asuka-FM achieves GenEval 0.588 / DPG 78.96 scores at 256px and GenEval 0.609 / DPG 78.51 scores at 512px, setting a new Pareto-optimal efficiency standard for democratic foundation modeling.

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

备注： 41 pages, 10 figures, Project page: [this https URL](https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network's weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator's next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: this https URL.

24. 【2611.26433】AdaPixFlow: Learning Continuous-Time 3D Dynamics Across Scenes from Sparse Multi-view Observations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26433

作者：Hrishikesh Viswanath,Aniket Bera

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：scenes requires recovering, unobserved times, Adaptive Pixel Flows, requires recovering, Understanding

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Understanding how objects move in dynamic scenes requires recovering their 3D motion from visual observations and predicting their positions at unobserved times. Existing methods either optimize scene-specific velocity fields or impose predefined physical and kinematic assumptions. We instead study learning a continuous-time dynamics operator across scenes, mapping multi-view visual observations to 3D object kinematics. We introduce Adaptive Pixel Flows (\ours{}), which learns this operator from multi-view RGB-D observations without per-scene optimization or predefined motion models. The method fuses observations from all cameras into a shared voxel grid in world coordinates, from which the network predicts a continuous-time velocity field. Because the operator is trained across scenes, it learns motion priors that carry over to unseen scenes. The field can then be integrated from observed states to predict object positions between observations and beyond the observed sequence. On held-out PhysInOne scenes, \ours{} improves F1 over the strongest competing method by 2.5\% for in-between prediction and 15\% for future prediction. Compared with per-scene velocity-field methods, the improvements are 30\% and 85\%, respectively. Applied zero-shot to Spring-Gaus, it improves future-prediction F1 by 12\%.

25. 【2611.26431】FFGaussType: Feed-Forward Conversion of Glyph and SVG Geometry into Compact Gaussian Assets

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26431

作者：Haoan Feng,Tianfu Wang,Peimeng Guan,Dae Yeol Lee,Guan-ming Su

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

备注： 20 pages. Project page: [this https URL](https://fengyee.github.io/FFGaussType/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Immersive 3D content allows text and graphical elements, such as subtitles, annotations, and icons, to be placed inside a scene rather than displayed as flat overlays. Placed this way, they respect occlusion and perspective and take on user-assigned materials and lighting while preserving their original shapes. In Gaussian scenes, however, each new glyph or icon requires its own iterative optimization, which limits scalable content creation. We present FFGaussType, which converts extruded glyph and SVG outlines into Gaussian assets in a single forward pass. A point encoder maps anchors sampled on the known surface to a single feature plane, from which a decoder shared by all assets predicts neural Gaussians carrying surface normals. This separates geometric conversion from material and lighting choices, and the feature plane supports image and video coding for compact delivery. We construct a paired dataset of 27,221 glyph and SVG icon assets with anchors and calibrated normal and silhouette renderings, holding out font families for evaluation. Conversion from prepared anchors takes 9.1 ms per asset, at a fraction of the compute and memory of prior baselines. On unseen font families and test icons, FFGaussType achieves $1.62^\circ$ normal error and $0.985$ silhouette IoU, the best among the compared feed-forward converters, matching the fidelity that per-asset fitting needs thousands of steps to reach. The code and dataset will be released after publication.

26. 【2611.26427】MemFolder: Structure-Guided Memory Folding for Efficient Agent Memory Management

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26427

作者：Xuyang Liu,Chenwen Lu,Junxi Wang,Siyu Ren,Xinying Lin,Siteng Huang,Hongli Sun,Linfeng Zhang,Honggang Chen

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Long-term agents accumulate, agents accumulate large, accumulate large external, Long-term agents, large external memory

备注： Code is available at [this https URL](https://github.com/xuyang-liu16/MemFolder)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long-term agents accumulate large external memory stores as interactions unfold, increasing the costs of storage, retrieval, and response generation. Efficiency is constrained by two coupled factors: a growing record count increases retrieval overhead, while longer retrieved contexts increase prefill computation and decoding-time attention costs. Existing methods prune or consolidate records, but pruning can discard useful evidence, whereas consolidation may produce fewer yet longer retrieval units. We introduce MemFolder, a structure-guided memory folding framework that sequentially reduces record count and context length. The framework comprises two core components that share memory groups, using cross-record structure to guide both evidence consolidation and compact representation. RecordFolder grounds entity mentions, progressively selects representative yet complementary anchors, and combines semantic and entity affinity with temporal chaining to consolidate compatible records into fewer retrieval units. ContextFolder encodes the original records within these groups into compact text and uses verification feedback to revise encodings when key facts cannot be recovered. Experiments on the three text-based memory benchmarks LoCoMo, PersonaMem, and LongMemEval and the two multimodal benchmarks M3-Bench-web and M3-Bench-robot demonstrate the strong performance of our methods. Notably, on Mem0, RecordFolder retains 99.0% of the original average performance across the three text-based benchmarks with 50% fewer records, exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.9 percentage points in performance retention. Combined with ContextFolder, existing record compression methods further shorten their output records while retaining most of their performance.

30. 【2611.26405】acInno: Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models the Contact Dynamics Beyond Vision and Action

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently been equipped with tactile sensing to perceive visually subtle contact events in contact-rich manipulation. However, adding tactile input yields inconsistent gains, and some visuo-tactile VLA models rely little on tactile input, because much of the tactile change, such as the gel deformation during gripper closure, is predictable from visual observations and executed actions. To address this problem, we introduce TacInno, which isolates the contact dynamics that vision and executed actions cannot explain, termed tactile innovation, and uses tactile innovation only as a training target. Ablation studies validate the contributions of visual and action conditioning, multi-frame encoding, and auxiliary code prediction. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile innovation as a supervision signal for contact-rich manipulation.

Abstract:Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. 32. 【2611.26388】GateTrace: Where Do Crowd Crossings Go Missing Under Occlusion?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26388

作者：Ao He,Qianchen Yu,Vivian W. H. Wong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：tracking supports entrance, pedestrian tracking supports, large events, passageway counts, tracking supports

备注： 23 pages, including supplementary material; 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:At large events, pedestrian tracking supports entrance and passageway counts, but totals hide which crossings were missed. In a stadium study, the system recorded about four in five clearly visible crossings but fewer than one in three mostly obscured crossings. The videos lacked trajectories annotated across frames, so we turned to MOT20, which provides pedestrian trajectories and visibility. We introduce GateTrace to locate missed crossings and assess changes to counting rules. It derives reference crossings from annotations under fixed rules, matches reports by person, direction, time and position, and traces misses through saved boxes and counting decisions. With a shared detector, ByteTrack, OC-SORT and BoT-SORT recover 38.9-46.7% of low-visibility reference crossings on development sequences, versus 98.1-99.0% at high visibility. The gap recurs on two further sequences under a protocol fixed in advance. Published outputs of MOT20-tuned BASE recover 63-78% at low visibility across both sequence groups. Losses stop at different stages across systems. Across four systems and both groups, 47-79% of low-visibility misses have no nearby output linked to the person or no possible crossing in the saved tracks. Removing later checks from BASE on development sequences leaves most misses unresolved and lowers precision against the fixed reference list from about 96% to 77%. Misses also remain among sampled reference crossings that two human annotators supported after checking the videos. GateTrace shows where crossings are lost and measures the recovery and precision costs of changing later checks. We will release the code and per-crossing records.

Abstract:External-eye photography captures clinically relevant periocular anatomy at low acquisition cost, yet no large-scale standardized resource exists for evaluating self-supervised external-eye representations. 34. 【2611.26380】EgoCaptioner: Towards Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Egocentric Video Captioning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26380

关键词：captioning requires fine-grained, requires fine-grained detail, current multimodal large, multimodal large language, video captioning requires

备注： 17 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Egocentric video captioning requires fine-grained detail and accurate timing, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often miss brief actions, confuse their order, or misinterpret the wearer's motion. Existing caption formats also offer limited support for combining detailed narration with precise timing. We present EgoCaptioner, which pairs a video-level overview of the setting, participants, and the wearer's overall activity with timed steps whose narration adapts to varying perceptual demands and marks key moments at specific timestamps. We develop a coarse-to-fine annotation pipeline that establishes event structure before expanding step descriptions, yielding 50,000 training examples from eight egocentric video sources. Complementary rewards assess factual accuracy and coverage, measure step-level temporal alignment, and penalize fragmented or redundant narration, guiding caption refinement through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To improve the reliability of factual supervision, we supplement caption references with pose-derived motion facts when sensor data are available. Across five egocentric benchmarks, EgoCaptioner achieves leading performance among evaluated open-source models in caption quality, caption-conditioned question answering, and temporal alignment. It also surpasses Gemini 3.1 Pro on PhysCapBench and all evaluated proprietary baselines on Minerva-Ego under the caption-to-QA protocol. Ablations support the complementary roles of factual and temporal rewards and show that coarse-to-fine annotation provides more effective supervision than single-pass annotation.

35. 【2611.26370】Fast Self-Supervised Generative CT Denoising via Few-Step Sampling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26370

作者：Xingyi Yao,Zhengtao Ma,Zhennong Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：obscures anatomical detail, thin-slice computed tomography, computed tomography, anatomical detail, suffer from elevated

备注： 30 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. Xingyi Yao and Zhengtao Ma contributed equally to this work. Zhennong Chen is the corresponding author

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Low-dose and thin-slice computed tomography (CT) images suffer from elevated noise that obscures anatomical detail, while clean references for supervised denoising are often unavailable. Although Noise2Noise permits self-supervised training from noisy pairs, direct regression tends to oversmooth fine structure. Averaging multiple independently generated samples mitigates this blur, though repeating multi-step diffusion sampling is costly. We introduce Noise2Noise with Improved Mean Flow (N2N-iMF) to accelerate this process via few-step sampling. N2N-iMF learns to generate one noisy image conditioned on its paired counterpart; at inference, independent outputs are sampled and averaged to suppress noise. Across eight PCCT cases, N2N-iMF reached a mean gray-white matter contrast-to-noise ratio of 1.341, versus 0.916 for Noise2Noise and 1.176 for DDIM. N2N-iMF accelerates self-supervised generative CT denoising without distillation while keeping the number of averaged outputs adjustable at inference.

37. 【2611.26347】OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26347

作者：Chi-Wei Lee,An-Kai Chen,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,Po-Chih Kuo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：PSNR, dynamic-region PSNR, Spacetime Gaussians, frame, synchronized

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://arthur031221.github.io/offbeat/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:For 4D reconstruction, a synchronized multi-camera rig records all views at the same timestamps and misses the motion between them. Staggered triggers spread the same frames over more timestamps. However, when the cameras along each row fire one after another, as in 4DSloMo's schedule on the N3DV rig, the row acts as one moving camera that confounds depth with motion. We introduce OffBeat, which models a staggered rig as a spatially distributed multicoset sampler: shifted copies of one sampling grid. From the calibration alone, OffBeat derives camera groups that fire together and their firing order. Since one group records each timestamp, a short-lived primitive can fit an error its cameras share, such as a color offset, and flicker in novel views. We show that a primitive's opacity over time weights all groups equally, for every number of groups, only if it lasts at least one camera frame interval. On Spacetime Gaussians, a minimum lifetime approximates this bound, and a closed-form smoothed box of that length satisfies it exactly, with less flicker than a Gaussian in its place at nearly equal PSNR. In simulated staggered capture on N3DV, OffBeat outperforms the full 4DSloMo pipeline with video diffusion on every test frame, by 3.04 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR. Between the first and last synchronized frame, it outperforms the best synchronized method of each scene, with or without frame interpolation, by 1.98 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR, while synchronized capture is better at its own frames. On PanopticSports, in this frame range, OffBeat is comparable in PSNR to synchronized capture with interpolation. On every N3DV scene, replacing 4DSloMo's schedule with ours raises the dynamic-region PSNR of Spacetime Gaussians, 4DSloMo without diffusion, and our reconstruction. Multi-camera rigs could follow faster motion at the same frame count by changing only when each camera fires.

38. 【2611.26334】Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26334

作者：Ryan Lucas,Petros Prastakos,Rahul Mazumder

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：transformers reuse key, diffusion transformers reuse, long video generation, Video diffusion transformers, reuse key

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video diffusion transformers reuse key--value (KV) caches across denoising steps, which quickly becomes a bottleneck for long video generation. We find that quantizing temporally distant cache vectors leads to a large range and high quantization error, but that nearby video tokens in the KV-cache share a persistent pattern per channel. These patterns also vary across videos, motivating rotations that are adapted to each video's unique cache. We propose Temporal Rotation Quantization (TRQ), a method that rotates frames in the cache that are adjacent in time before quantization. For each block of temporally adjacent cache vectors, TRQ fits an orthogonal transform that maps the block's per-channel means to a vector that is close to a constant across channels. To keep the quantization format lightweight, we parameterize this rotation as a tree of $2 \times 2$ planar rotations, each stored with only two bits, followed by a fixed Hadamard transform shared across all blocks. The rotations are fit without gradients or changes to model weights, and we prove that a simple greedy fit is globally optimal for our 'mean-flattening' objective. The transforms are fit online for each new video, and we release fused Triton kernels to make this process efficient at inference. At 2-bit precision and matched compression, TRQ improves VBench subject consistency over the state-of-the-art method QVG from 90.38 to 92.02 on LongCat-Video-13B and from 86.33 to 87.61 on HY-WorldPlay-8B, as well as improving other metrics.

39. 【2611.26321】FirstCross: Convex Single-Photon 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26321

作者：Chi-Wei Lee,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,An-Kai Chen,Po-Chih Kuo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：Single-photon lidar records, lidar records, records the arrival, photons, Single-photon lidar

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://arthur031221.github.io/firstcross/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Single-photon lidar records the arrival time of each returning photon, enabling 3D reconstruction from few views and scarce photons. However, leading neural methods such as Transientangelo require over an hour of non-convex per-scene optimization, and their accuracy degrades as photon flux decreases. We propose FirstCross, a network-free approach that instead solves a short sequence of convex problems on a voxel grid. For a solid object, the Poisson likelihood of a ray with given signal and background rates depends on the scene solely through its first crossing, the point where the ray first enters the object. Decomposing this likelihood yields two tractable terms. The first penalizes surfaces in front of detected photons and admits a tight convex closure, namely volume rendering with a running-maximum transmittance in place of NeRF's product transmittance. The second accounts for photons that no surface explains and admits a convex upper bound once the object is assumed to be thick behind each detected surface point. Compared with Transientangelo under its own real-data protocol, FirstCross achieves lower depth error across every test scene, view count, and flux level. With three views and 100 times fewer photons than at the protocol's lowest flux, our mean object depth error stays under half of Transientangelo's with all photons. Given our detected surface points, the best classical fusion has 1.3 to 1.8 times our three-view error across flux levels. On real room-scale captures with 1% of their photons, TransientNeRF has 2.8 to 7.7 times our error. FirstCross lets few-view single-photon scanners cut exposure to 1% and reconstruct an object in about a minute on one GPU.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26318

作者：Guojian Zhan,Jiahao Xu,Yahui Liu,Yao Hu,Chuan Mu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic visual reasoning improves vision-language models by selectively enlarging evidence without adding new scene content, but the benefit depends on whether fine structure remains accessible and whether local observations stay grounded in the full scene. We identify three recurring bottlenecks: high-frequency detail can be attenuated during visual-token formation; spatially distributed evidence can remain encoded yet difficult for the language model to retrieve; and isolated crops can weaken whole-detail correspondence through scale and positional mismatch. ResVision addresses these limitations with two complementary components. Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR) estimates image complexity and selects an adaptive global sampling scale, avoiding unnecessary enlargement on simple inputs while allocating more visual tokens to detail-rich images. Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI) returns each inspected crop together with the complete working view, preserving local detail and global context across tool turns. Complete ResVision (Agentic Base plus SMR plus CPI) is best for every model, improving by 3.36 to 4.94 points over Direct Base and 1.87 to 2.94 points over Agentic Base. 48. 【2611.26234】Attention Drift Regularization with Adaptive Initialization for Domain-Robust Visual Recognition

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26234

作者：Shivangi Rai,Vinod K Kurmi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Source-free domain adaptation, modes remain unaddressed, critical failure modes, failure modes remain, source model

备注： Accepted in WACV 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a pretrained source model to unlabeled target domains without source data access, yet two critical failure modes remain unaddressed. First, attention drift: source-learned attention maps shift toward spurious or background features under distribution shift, destabilizing feature representations. Second, domain-gap-agnostic initialization: applying a single fixed source model uniformly across all target domains ignores domain gap severity, causing premature overfitting to noisy pseudo-labels under large shifts. We propose DGA-CAFC to address both failures. The Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC) module enforces feature stability by minimizing the discrepancy between representations computed under learned attention and those under counterfactual-inspired normalized perturbations, thereby promoting domain-robust features independent of spurious attention shifts. The Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS) module estimates domain gap severity via target prediction entropy and selects the source model variant whose confidence level best matches the estimated gap, reducing negative transfer and stabilizing pseudo-label quality. Crucially, CAFC transfers beyond SFDA: applied as a lightweight regularizer to standard domain generalization objectives without target data. Extensive experiments across SFDA and DG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves over recent baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework. 51. 【2611.26201】Learn, Freeze, and Transfer an Activation Function Across Architectures

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26201

作者：Arun Prakash A

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：typically reuses knowledge, reuses knowledge acquired, trained from scratch, learning typically reuses, Transfer learning typically

备注： 17 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/AI4Bharat/LeFT-activation-transfer) , Interactive figures: [this https URL](https://arunprakash-a.github.io/papers/learn-freeze-transfer/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transfer learning typically reuses knowledge acquired by a source network through its learned weights. We investigate whether a learned activation function can also be transferred and remain useful in networks trained from scratch. We study this complementary form of transfer through a learn-freeze-transfer protocol, in which the activation is optimized jointly with a source network in a single training run, and is then frozen and reused in target networks trained from scratch. A pointwise activation with globally shared parameters is independent of layer dimensions and can therefore be incorporated into networks of different architectures trained from scratch. We instantiate this protocol with FAct, a five-parameter activation represented by a two-harmonic truncated Fourier series, initialized from a Fourier approximation of GELU and learned in a Vision Transformer (ViT) on ImageNet-1K. When reused in a ViT of the same architecture trained from scratch on ImageNet-1K, the frozen activation (Frozen FAct) improves top-1 accuracy by 1.46 percentage points over GELU. Across four smaller target architectures (standard ViT, Swin Transformer, linear-attention ViT, and MLP-Mixer) and four image-classification datasets, it achieves the highest mean accuracy among eleven activation functions in 15 of 16 settings under a common training configuration, with improvements of up to 1.71 percentage points over the best conventional activation. When the learning rate and weight decay are tuned separately for each activation under the same search budget, Frozen FAct still ranks first in 11 of the 16 settings, second in four, and fourth in one. These results provide empirical evidence that a learned activation function can retain its usefulness when transferred across vision architectures, independently of the weights with which it was learned.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Thinking with images (TwI) empowers vision-language models (VLMs) to overcome perceptual bottlenecks by actively cropping and zooming into fine-grained visual details, promising both higher accuracy and interpretable, region-grounded reasoning. However, our analysis shows that the improved accuracy of TwI models does not guarantee faithful tool use; they frequently invoke tools without actually relying on the zoomed region, undermining the reliability of tool-assisted reasoning. We trace this unfaithful behaviour to the training data: when questions are answerable without zooming, the model can answer correctly while ignoring the returned evidence, and this problem is exacerbated during training as the model becomes better at answering without tools. To keep zooming useful throughout training, we adapt the training data to the model's evolving capability and train on questions where correct zoomed evidence can still help the model answer correctly. This selection is supported by a data synthesis pipeline that localises small objects and constructs verifiable questions about their fine details, and complemented by an adaptiveness reward that favours tool-free answers when zooming is unnecessary. The experimental results show that our method substantially improves reasoning faithfulness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual search benchmarks (+11.6% on V*, +7.9% on HR-Bench 4K, and +9.4% on HR-Bench 8K). On multimodal hallucination benchmarks, our model also outperforms the compared TwI baselines. These results suggest that improving TwI faithfulness does not sacrifice accuracy and that both can be learnt from the right training data without directly supervising the reasoning process.

54. 【2611.26191】Backbone Matters: Benchmarking Encoder-Decoder Networks for Semantic Segmentation of Driving Scenes on BDD100K

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26191

作者：Ehsan Rassekh

备注： 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Semantic segmentation gives an autonomous vehicle a dense, pixel-level description of its surroundings and is a core part of scene understanding. Most segmentation networks consist of an encoder (the backbone) and a decoder, yet the backbone is often chosen without a systematic comparison. This work studies this choice on the BDD100K driving dataset. Four widely used decoders (U-Net, FPN, LinkNet and PSPNet) are combined with 13 backbones from six families (ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet and EfficientNet), and all 52 models are trained with one common protocol. 55. 【2611.26187】Invertible Transport Between Sample-Only Distributions via Projected Characteristic Functions

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26187

作者：Juan Gutiérrez,Victor Gutiérrez-García,José Luis Blanco-Murillo

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：PCF, latent distributions, tractable density, MMD, kernel MMD

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Many useful latent distributions can be sampled but do not have a tractable density. Such distributions may be represented by empirical collections of real-world observations or generated by scientific simulators and black-box generative models. These sample-only priors cannot be used directly in likelihood-based normalizing flows, which require evaluating the prior density. We propose Projected Characteristic-Function matching (PCF), a density-free framework for learning an invertible map between a data distribution and a prior specified only through samples. Our method aligns distributions using projected empirical characteristic functions, eliminating the need for density estimation, adversarial training, or paired observations. To address the non-uniqueness of the transport map, we introduce an optional transport regularizer that selects lower-displacement transformations while preserving endpoint alignment. We benchmark PCF on controlled transports, sample-efficiency studies, and high-dimensional alignment tasks. PCF matches the accuracy of kernel MMD on the controlled benchmark and on raw-pixel MNIST--USPS at a cost linear in the batch size, and it is more sample-efficient, achieving lower endpoint error than MMD in 13 of 15 paired runs across library sizes. By avoiding pairwise kernel evaluation, it reduces discrepancy memory by 29.8% and trains at batch sizes where kernel MMD exceeds GPU memory. These results show that PCF provides accurate and efficient invertible transport, particularly in sample- and memory-limited settings.

56. 【2611.26179】RegionAgent: Two-Stage Preference Optimization for Regional Image Restoration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26179

作者：Weiyu Zhou,Jiacong Tang,Tao Hu,Axi Niu,Jinqiu Sun,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：non-uniform coupled degradations, diverse restoration capabilities, restoration, non-uniform coupled, coupled degradations require

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Images under non-uniform coupled degradations require diverse restoration capabilities for different degraded regions. Existing methods employ globally uniform operations, potentially overlooking regional differences and causing incomplete restoration or even damage to image content. Moreover, a degraded region may require successive restoration actions, and earlier actions may alter residual degradation characteristics or introduce artifacts, potentially hindering subsequent restoration. To address these challenges, we propose RegionAgent, an adaptive restoration framework that trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) through two-stage preference learning to generate a region-specific restoration action at each iteration. Specifically, we first train the VLM to select an action suited to the current region through step-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), using preferences derived from immediate restoration feedback to supervise only the differing component in each action pair. To account for the effects of current actions on subsequent restoration, we then apply trajectory-level DPO, using preferences derived from complete trajectory scores to supervise the sole differing action in each trajectory pair. Furthermore, as conventional image-pair datasets do not directly provide preferences over regional restoration decisions, we construct a regional restoration preference dataset comprising 10K action pairs and 20K trajectory pairs for two-stage VLM training. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate strong restoration performance under both mixed and single degradations. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of two-stage preference learning in improving restoration quality.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26178

作者：Ji Wang,Haonan An,Jiasheng Zhang,Renlong Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：gained rapid momentum, live content creation, interactive media, generation has gained, gained rapid

备注： 23 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-time streaming video generation has gained rapid momentum in live content creation and interactive media. Deploying such systems in practice requires flexible scaling factors to accommodate diverse source resolutions, target displays, and computational budgets. However, extending fixed-scale generative video super-resolution (VSR) to varying target scales poses two challenges: downsampling from a larger fixed scale incurs redundant cost, while re-optimizing the diffusion transformer (DiT) is costly and alters the shared generative prior. To address both, we propose FlexVSR, a decoupled framework for continuous-scale streaming generative VSR with supervision at only a few scales. Our key insight is that naively resampling fixed-scale latents onto a new target grid induces scale-dependent input mismatch, which can be effectively addressed through parameter-efficient, scale-aware adaptation of the latent interface without updating the DiT. To this end, FlexVSR introduces ReLSA (Refined Latent Scale Adaptation), a lightweight latent adapter that constructs and refines target-grid latents before they enter the DiT, enabling a shared frozen VSR prior to generalize across continuous scales with only 0.274M trainable parameters and an adaptation cost of 7.3 H100 GPU-hours, while introducing no additional temporal dependencies and preserving the native causal streaming protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive or superior reconstruction and temporal quality, lower latency than most native-scale streaming baselines and offline methods, and effective generalization to unseen continuous scales, filling the continuous-scale gap in real-time streaming generative VSR.

备注： ACM Multimedia 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Personalized video generation faces a significant scalability challenge: as the number of custom subjects increases, existing methods suffer from severe identity leakage and semantic entanglement, resulting in videos with blended attributes and incoherent narratives. We identify that this stems from a lack of explicit binding between textual entity phrases and their corresponding visual patterns prior to fusion in diffusion models. To address this, we propose MultiBinder, a novel framework built on a ``bind-then-narrate'' paradigm. Our approach introduces a two-stage conditioning process before video diffusion. These features are then injected into a video diffusion model via dedicated conditioning pathways, ensuring precise attribute allocation. Extensive experiments on both general and newly curated multi-subject benchmarks show that MultiBinder significantly outperforms existing methods in multi-subject identity fidelity.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pathology foundation models supply strong representations for whole-slide image classification, yet the confidence attached to their predictions remains hard to trust. Prevailing uncertainty estimators act within a single feature space and thereby overlook the uncertainty that the choice of foundation-model representation itself induces. This work introduces CALIFUSE, a calibration-oriented framework that fuses several pathology foundation models for the specific purpose of uncertainty estimation. Departing from accuracy-driven fusion, CALIFUSE reads representational heterogeneity across encoders as an explicit confidence cue. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26145

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World models can imagine how a physical scene evolves over the next few seconds, but growers need to know how a crop will develop over weeks to months: will this rice plant have headed a month from now, and how will it look at harvest? We introduce agricultural world modeling, the task of predicting how the same plant will look at a requested horizon of days to months, from one photo of it, its cultivar, and its camera view. In preliminary analysis, even state-of-the-art image generators get this wrong: they often grow the plant too little or give it the color and form of the wrong growth stage. On maize, a crop with a different body plan and stage scale, the same design again scores best on all four measures.

64. 【2611.26128】CryoGaussian: Heterogeneous Cryo-EM Reconstruction Made Explicit with Gaussian Splatting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26128

作者：Ziwen Wang,Jiajun Fan,Chaoran Cheng,Ruihan Guo,Yanru Qu,Juexiao Zhou,Ge Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 59 pages, 25 figures, 15 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) determines macromolecular structures from noisy particle images. However, rare, transient compositional states and subtle, continuous conformational variation are difficult to resolve. We present CryoGaussian, a Gaussian splatting method for heterogeneous cryo-EM reconstruction that fits an explicit Gaussian density representation directly to the particle images through a differentiable rasterizer rendering orthographic line integrals modulated by the contrast transfer function. The Gaussians are initialized randomly, with their number derived from the estimated spatial support of the molecular density and their minimum size bounded by the Nyquist frequency of the particle images. To capture the compositional and conformational variation across particle images, we learn particle-image and per-Gaussian latents that jointly modulate the base position, amplitude, and scale of each Gaussian. Each structural state is then reconstructed by fitting the Gaussians conditioned on its inferred latent. On CryoBench, CryoGaussian achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy for both compositional and conformational heterogeneity. On experimental data, it resolves a rare compositional state of the 50S ribosomal subunit (class C4, 1.3% of particles) with rRNA helix 68 docked in the absence of the central protuberance, and continuous conformational motion of the SF3b and helicase subcomplexes of the pre-catalytic spliceosome. We further optimize the rasterizer for additive cryo-EM projection, reducing training time by 12.8-28.0$\times$.

Abstract:Video-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on video-text retrieval and are increasingly used for egocentric video understanding. We demonstrate that this exposure reweighting can improve video-text retrieval without stronger verb-noun compositional understanding, and that the gain depends on the correct sample-level exposure correspondence. Abstract:The proliferation of AI-generated videos has raised growing concerns about misinformation, fraud, and potential societal risks, creating an urgent need for detection. Reconstruction-error-based methods have shown strong empirical performance for AI-generated image detection by exploiting discrepancies between an input and its reconstructed version. 68. 【2611.26103】UniVEP: Unifying Velocity and Endpoint Prediction for Robot Manipulation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26103

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Flow-matching robot policies usually use one prediction target throughout action generation, even though recovering actions from noise and polishing nearly clean actions bring different challenges. We systematically compare velocity prediction (V), endpoint prediction (X), and velocity-weighted endpoint prediction (X--V) across multiple robot policies, benchmarks, and inference budgets. On StarVLA and LiLa-WAM, X--V works best for few-step generation, while V gains more from iterative refinement. 69. 【2611.26098】Frequency-Synchronized Boundary Coupling for Training-Free Multi-Prompt Long Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26098

作者：Yipan Xu,Shuyong Gao,Jiyuan Fu,Keliang Yin,Jiayu Chen,Qianyu Guo,Yang Liu,Wenqiang Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：descriptions remains challenging, made remarkable progress, evolving textual descriptions, textual descriptions remains, transformer-based video architectures

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Project page: [this https URL](https://yipanxu-22.github.io/FreqSync-project)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-video generation has made remarkable progress with diffusion models and transformer-based video architectures, yet generating coherent long videos from evolving textual descriptions remains challenging. Training long-video models from scratch requires substantial computation and large-scale multi-event data, while training-free multi-prompt generation must activate new prompt semantics without breaking temporal continuity. We identify a frequency-agnostic boundary coupling problem in existing transition strategies: weak coupling causes abrupt changes, flicker, and background jumps, whereas overly strong or coarse coupling may over-preserve source semantics and produce ghosting or duplicated subjects around prompt boundaries. To address this problem, we propose FreqSync, a training-free framework that formulates prompt transitions as frequency-selective boundary coupling. FreqSync combines Frequency-Synchronized Source-Conditioned Attention (FS-SCA) with Residual-Clipped Spectral Stitching (RCSS), enabling adjacent segments to share stable low- and mid-frequency structure and motion cues while keeping high-frequency prompt-specific details target-dominant under explicit residual constraints. Extensive experiments show that FreqSync achieves state-of-the-art transition smoothness and prompt alignment while maintaining strong perceptual video quality, demonstrating its effectiveness for coherent multi-prompt long video generation.

70. 【2611.26072】DfD-GS: Defocus Image Prior for Metric-Scale Single-View Depth Estimation and View Synthesis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26072

作者：Shiori Ueda,Rinto Yagawa,Hideo Saito,Shohei Mori

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recovering metric-scale geometry, remains fundamentally ill-posed, single viewpoint remains, viewpoint remains fundamentally, Recovering metric-scale

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://sh1027.github.io/dfd-gs/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recovering metric-scale geometry from observations at a single viewpoint remains fundamentally ill-posed, limiting the reliability of novel view synthesis. Recent methods rely on learned monocular depth priors, which provide strong structural estimates but can suffer from scale ambiguity and dataset-dependent biases. We propose DfD-GS, a feed-forward framework that predicts a metric-scale 3D Gaussian scene representation from a single-view all-in-focus--defocused image pair. The key idea is to exploit defocus blur as a physical metric depth cue. DfD-GS first estimates a defocus-based metric disparity map from the all-in-focus--defocused image pair and the corresponding defocus strength. Qualitative results on a real dataset show recovery of the main scene structure under optical defocus.

71. 【2611.26065】Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065

作者：Nidhal Eddine Chenni,Arunkumar Rathinam,Djamila Aouada

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：proximity operations require, Autonomous rendezvous, accurate estimate, target spacecraft, rendezvous and proximity

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations require not only an accurate estimate of the pose of the target spacecraft, but also a reliable indication of when this estimate can be trusted. Conformal prediction provides such an indication with a formal guarantee, by producing pose uncertainty sets that contain the true pose with a specified probability. Such a guarantee, however, assumes that calibration and test data follow the same distribution, an assumption that is violated in orbital perception, where labelled calibration images are available only from synthetic renderings while operational images are real. In this work, we show on the SPEED+ dataset that conformal pose estimation calibrated on synthetic images loses its coverage guarantee on real images, and that many of the resulting failures are silent: the predicted pose set appears valid but excludes the true pose. To address this, we calibrate on synthetic images translated to the appearance of real images. Since translation alters appearance while preserving geometry, the synthetic pose labels remain valid and no labelled real images are required. Experiments with two translation models show that this calibration restores coverage above the specified level for miscoverage levels of 0.1 and above, and reduces silent failures from up to 43.5% to at most 3.3% of the test images, bringing conformal pose estimation a step closer to trustworthy deployment in space.

72. 【2611.26058】One-to-Many: Diverse and Bidirectional Image-to-Image Translation via Rectified Flow

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26058

作者：Sho Shimotsumagari,Seiichi Uchida,Shumpei Takezaki

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 12 pages, 13 figures, 4 tables. Includes supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Many paired image-to-image translation tasks admit multiple valid target appearances, while training provides only one target per source. We propose One-to-Many Flow (O2MFlow), a latent-conditioned Rectified Flow (RF) model for one-to-many generation and backward translation (target$\rightarrow$source) within a single transport framework. Conditioning the RF dynamics on an appearance latent turns transport into a family of latent-indexed bijections: a fixed latent gives a mapping that is inverted by time-reversed integration, and sampled latents produce diverse outputs for the same input. We formalize reusable appearance variations as Shared Latent Transport and show that Kullback--Leibler (KL) regularization directly controls the source--latent distribution gap between training and generation. Across three benchmarks, O2MFlow offers dataset-dependent forward diversity--fidelity trade-offs and the lowest backward LPIPS-Dist among evaluated methods, with the lowest backward FID on two benchmarks.

74. 【2611.26045】PaveSplat: Resolution-Robust Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting with Residually Corrected Surface-Aligned Covariances

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26045

作者：Janghun Kim,Hyun-Cheol Kim,Eunbin An,Myeonghun Oh,Hyon-Gon Choo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：feed-forward Gaussian splatting, Gaussian splatting method, Pixel-aligned feed-forward Gaussian, keeping surfels paving, Gaussian splatting predicts

备注： Accepted to ACCV 2026. 16 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://janghunn.github.io/pavesplat/) ; code: [this https URL](https://github.com/janghunn/pavesplat)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present PaveSplat, a feed-forward Gaussian splatting method that adds a learned residual to the analytic surface-aligned covariance, keeping surfels paving the visible surface across rendering resolutions. Pixel-aligned feed-forward Gaussian splatting predicts one primitive per pixel from a few images in a single pass, and its rendering quality depends on how well these primitives cover the surface. Recent surfel-based methods compute the surface-aligned covariance analytically from predicted depth. However, this covariance has no way to correct the surfel orientation where the depth-derived frame is ill-posed. The surfels then cover the surface loosely; at the training resolution each surfel's one-pixel minimum footprint covers the gaps, but they show up as holes when the same prediction is re-rendered at a higher resolution. To address this, we keep the analytic covariance as an initialization, with its axes aligned to the surface tangents, and add an identity-initialized unit-quaternion residual that reorients a surfel only where this improves rendering, turning surfels that the analytic frame mis-aims back onto the surface. On RealEstate10K, PaveSplat achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among single-Gaussian-per-pixel methods at every scale (1x-8x). The native-resolution gain is small; the benefit appears when the same prediction is rendered at higher display resolutions without re-running the network. On zero-shot DL3DV, it leads PSNR within this group at every scale and ranks first or second on SSIM/LPIPS. A coverage-hole analysis shows fewer uncovered pixels than both baselines under high-resolution re-rendering.

Abstract:World models aim to predict environment dynamics under actions, enabling the prediction of action consequences without executing them in the real world. This limits their use in tasks such as robotic policy evaluation and model-based planning, where nearly identical actions can lead to qualitatively different outcomes. Abstract:Editing a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture of a person so that they wear a new garment requires every rendered view to agree on the same garment details. The resulting inconsistencies are baked into the 3D representation and surface as texture drift, blurred prints, and implausible geometry in novel views. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946

作者：Dong An,Tianyu Liu,Haoran Ding,Wen Yang,Anqing Duan,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：models predict future, predict future video, Robotic world models, world models predict, predict future

备注： 24 pages, 7 figures, 11 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://marsaki.github.io/point-dynamics-proxy/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We attribute this to the conditioning: an action, or a rendering of the robot, specifies where the robot goes but not how the scene responds, leaving a single video model to infer the interaction dynamics implicitly while also rendering appearance. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. Specifically, we lift the first frame into 3D points, simulate how they respond to the robot's motion with a learned point dynamics model, and render the simulated points back into pixel-aligned videos that any video model can read. In controlled experiments on the DROID dataset with two video renderers of different architectures, this point dynamics proxy reduces object motion error by 8.6% and 6.2% without degrading image quality, and by 8.1% on a real bimanual robot. Moreover, the video follows the simulation: more accurate dynamics yield more accurate object motion, and editing the points steers the video. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but Gaussian rasterization attenuates fine-scale scene structure through the projected covariance of each primitive. We show that contracting these projected covariances at inference time can mitigate the effective bandwidth limitation induced by finite Gaussian footprints, motivating a multi-aperture rendering framework for super-resolution. Based on this insight, we propose AGSR (Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution), a lightweight inference-time framework that progressively fuses multi-aperture renderings using uncertainty-guided feature aggregation and geometric conditioning from transmittance and depth cues. AGSR operates as a plug-in enhancement for pre-trained 3DGS models. Experiments on the Mip-NeRF 360 benchmark show that AGSR outperforms lightweight image-space super-resolution baselines, with gains increasing as the underlying Gaussian representation deteriorates. The results provide evidence that covariance contraction exposes bandwidth-sensitive structural cues attenuated by native Gaussian rasterization. AGSR achieves these improvements while maintaining real-time performance with up to 139k parameters.

84. 【2611.25930】Why Flat Drafts Stay Flat: Region-Mean Latents Govern Texture in Flow-Based Image Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25930

作者：Zihan Gu,Jiangcan Jia,Hua Zhang,Lulu Zhao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：interior detail unspecified, leaving interior detail, pretrained flow-based generators, detail unspecified, photorealistic prompts

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Flat color-block drafts specify layout and regional color while leaving interior detail unspecified, yet pretrained flow-based generators can preserve their flat appearance even under photorealistic prompts. We trace this behavior to a cross-scale mechanism linking fine pixel variation to regional means in variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. Flattening photo regions systematically shifts their latent means, with offsets concentrated along a shared channel direction within each backbone. Controlled field exchanges in FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image establish the effect of this displacement on texture generation: adding the photo-derived regional mean field to a flat latent restores texture, whereas adding its complementary field alone largely preserves flatness. A second-order encoding expansion explains how encoder curvature can convert zero-mean perturbations into mean shifts. An analytically solvable conditional Gaussian flow shows how regional noise averaging enables near-unit endpoint sensitivity at high signal-to-noise ratios. This mechanism motivates training-free rendering through pixel dither before encoding, mean-field scaling, and early boundary holding. Paired low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with draft conditioning improves content recovery and produces photo-like regional means under the standard velocity objective. Explicit regional mean guidance remains effective after adaptation, continuously controlling texture and improving color fidelity. These findings connect encoding shifts to persistent generation behavior and support interventions evaluated for texture, spatial fidelity, and semantic recovery.

86. 【2611.25904】Multiple-Camera Tracking of Rigid Objects

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25904

作者：Frédérick Martin,Radu Horaud

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：tracking rigid objects, paper we describe, tracking rigid, rigid objects, image contours

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this paper we describe a method for tracking rigid objects using one or several cameras. The tracking process consists of aligning a 3-D model representation of an object with image contours by measuring and minimizing the image error between predicted model points and image contours. The tracker behaves like a visual servo loop where the internal and external camera parameters are updated at each new image acquisition. We study in detail the Jacobian matrix associated with this minimization process in the presence of both point-to-point and point-to-contour matches. We establish the minimal number of matches that are needed as well as the singular configurations leading to a rank-defficient Jacobian matrix. We find a mathematical link between the point-to-point and point-to-contour cases. Based on this link we show that the latter has the same kind of singularities than the former. Moreover, we study multiple camera configurations which optimize the robustness of the method in the presence of single-camera singularities, bad, noisy, or missing data. Extensive experiments done with a complex ship part and with up to three cameras validate the method. In particular we show that the tracker may well be used as a camera calibration procedure.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-supervised foundation models are often said to need fewer labels than supervised training. We test this on a forestry task: classifying 20 tree species classes in 0.2 m aerial images from the TreeSatAI dataset. We compare a frozen DINOv3 model pretrained on 493 million satellite images, with only a linear classifier trained on top, against an ImageNet ResNet-50 that is fine-tuned end to end. On the official split, fine-tuning wins clearly (macro F1 0.764 vs. 0.685). However, 28.8% of the official test patches overlap a training or validation patch, and copying the label of the nearest such patch is correct for 83.6% of them. We therefore build a spatial split that holds out whole 2 km squares with a 200 m buffer. On this split the comparison becomes inconclusive (0.648 vs. 0.640, paired 95% CI of the difference [-0.054, 0.016]), and the learning curves of the two models overlap at every label budget from 343 to 33,126 training patches. On the five rarest classes, the frozen model achieves a higher mean F1 (0.566 vs. 0.445), although the confidence interval for this difference includes zero ([-0.05, 0.23]). Diagnostics show that the frozen features mainly miss differences between closely related species, such as the two native oaks. Choosing the input size and layer on validation data (224 px, block 16) raises the frozen model to 0.662 on the spatial split. A comparison that looks decisive on the official split is thus inconclusive under geographically separated evaluation. We release the spatial split and our code.

88. 【2611.25889】EvoMem: Self-Evolving Skills for Time-Aware Long Video Memory Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25889

作者：Yutong Guan,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Bing Sun,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：global semantic matching, coherent evidence chain, state-driven temporal control, lack state-driven temporal, large vision-language models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Although memory-augmented architectures have expanded the ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) to process long videos, existing retrieval processes still largely rely on global semantic matching and lack state-driven temporal control, often returning fragmented or redundant evidence and failing to maintain a coherent evidence chain. To address this challenge, we propose EvoMem, a time-aware long-video memory agent guided by self-evolving retrieval skills. EvoMem constructs a hierarchical time-indexed memory, where different types of evidence are aligned onto a unified video timeline; during retrieval, temporal cues from acquired evidence are further converted into explicit signals that constrain subsequent memory access. To address recurring procedural errors in long-video question answering, such as unclear retrieval targets, improper memory access paths, or premature stopping, EvoMem introduces retrieval skills to provide process-level guidance, enabling the agent to dynamically adjust retrieval content, memory operations, and target temporal ranges according to the current evidence state, while deciding when to stop. Furthermore, inspired by reinforcement learning, we formulate skill evolution as a reward-guided policy improvement process. Each candidate skill is treated as an external policy patch, and rewards are assigned according to answer-correctness state transitions on the validation set. In this way, effective skills are accepted and low-quality skills are rejected without updating model parameters. Experiments on four long-video question answering benchmarks show that EvoMem significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 60.1% and an average improvement of 8.6 percentage points over the previous best methods, demonstrating its effectiveness for long-video reasoning.

Abstract:Recalling where and when an object was last seen and who saw it requires recognizing it across days and people. To address this gap, we present EgoWitness, a benchmark for instance-level spatial memory and reasoning in egocentric video. Its 8,453 human-verified questions span approximately 324 hours of week-long and short-horizon recordings and test seven abilities, from recognizing an object or room to recalling who saw it and reasoning about room visits. 92. 【2611.25848】Interpretable Prototype-Conditioned Adaptation for Weather Forecasting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25848

作者：Zhixuan Li,Hao Jiang,Chen Hui,Kai Geng,Weisi Lin

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：requires distinguishing local, distinguishing local states, local, forecasting requires distinguishing, requires distinguishing

备注： 38 pages, including supplementary material. Project page: [this https URL](https://zhixuanli.github.io/projects/li2026protocast)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Data-driven weather forecasting requires distinguishing local states and their temporal evolution. However, learned patterns remain implicit in neural forecasters, making their influence difficult to interpret. Failing to distinguish local evolution can lead to similar forecasts for locations that develop differently, increasing prediction error. This motivates explicit references for distinguishing and interpreting local patterns. Prototypes provide explicit pattern references, but sample- or task-level conditioning leaves open how to adapt to distinct locations and variable groups. To deal with this problem, we design ProtoCast, which uses group-specific prototype banks to adapt local features while accounting for cross-variable dependencies. Specifically, (1) we combine each group's historical features and temporal change with forecast context from cross-variable attention for evolution-aware prototype matching. (2) Sparse mixtures of prototype-specific low-rank operators tailor corrections to each group and location. A gated attention block then exchanges the corrected features across groups before prediction. Each group's correction decomposes into explicit prototype contributions before this fusion. (3) We jointly train the forecasting network and operators from scratch and refit each group's prototypes before every training epoch. Slot tracking preserves prototype-operator associations, making each prototype's contribution to local corrections more interpretable. Experiments on GlobalForecast and TyphoonTracking show improved forecasting accuracy, while prototype deletion links local feature contributions to changes in prediction error. This work advances the development of more accurate and interpretable data-driven weather forecasting models. Project page: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Flow matching enables high-quality image and video generation, but its iterative sampling process requires repeated evaluations of large neural networks. Existing few-step methods can distort the output and reduce its agreement with a many-step reference. We propose TrajCal, a training-free calibration framework for few-step sampling. 94. 【2611.25840】Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25840

作者：Qiming Huang,Hanze Dong,Jipeng Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xinxing Xu,Furu Wei

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：vision-language agents increasingly, agents increasingly rely, increasingly rely, recent work, work has focused

备注： 22 pages, 3 figures. Qiming Huang, Hanze Dong, and Jipeng Zhang contributed equally

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical vision-language agents increasingly rely on segmentation tools, and recent work has focused on acquiring the right mask, often treating a good mask as the end point of tool use. But do these agents use the resulting evidence effectively? To answer this question, we need a benchmark that separates evidence use from segmentation quality, spans multiple medical image types, and allows the same mask to be presented in different ways. Existing medical VQA datasets do not jointly provide these properties. We therefore construct Mask-Evidence MedVQA, a 2.5K-example benchmark spanning four image types, where each question is paired with an answer-relevant ground-truth mask and multiple possible renderings. Our study reveals two limitations of direct tool use. First, evidence presentation matters. Different renderings of the same fixed mask can substantially change model behavior. Second, masks introduce an evidence trade-off. Highlighting a relevant region can expose diagnostic evidence the model previously missed, but the added visual cue can also induce incorrect associations. As a result, even a ground-truth mask can correct some answers while turning initially correct ones into incorrect ones. Together, these findings show that acquiring the right mask is not enough. An agent must also decide how to present the evidence and whether it should change the answer. We therefore introduce TUNE (Tool Use is Not Enough), an evidence-management harness that explicitly manages what evidence to acquire, how to present it, and whether to accept the resulting answer update. Across three public medical VQA benchmarks, evidence management improves the fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B backbone's mean accuracy by 4.0 percentage points. Full TUNE improves over direct tool use by 9.1 points on average across seven benchmarks and achieves competitive results against existing medical VLMs and agents.

Abstract:Spurious correlations can give image predictors high in-distribution accuracy but poor generalization under distribution shift. 98. 【2611.25822】Limits of local curvature for constructing search subspaces in segmentation models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25822

作者：Maxim Bolshim(1),Alexander Kugaevskikh(1) ((1) ITMO University, Saint Petersburg, Russia)

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：semantic segmentation models, gradient update field, field change, semantic segmentation, initial gradient

备注： 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Includes an appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We examine how well subspaces constructed from local curvature represent the change in the gradient update field after a finite step in semantic segmentation models. The local response is the action of the curvature operator on the actual input displacement. In a study of 50 images and two models, we measure the ratio of the loss increase attained in a fixed subspace to that attained in the full space. This ratio decreases as the search radius increases. On ten images from the same sample, we compare the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the initial gradient with two rank-16 subspaces for both models. The two equal-rank subspaces are assessed at the same initial states, using the same actual steps and measured vectors. Adding generalised Gauss-Newton directions to the initial gradient reduces the mean relative error in representing the field change from 0.922 to 0.874 for the first model and from 0.819 to 0.763 for the second. At the same rank, including the response direction for the initial gradient nearly eliminates the error in representing the local response. The mean error in representing the field change does not decrease. A decomposition with fixed pixel weights shows that the finite-step remainder is comparable in norm to the field change and makes the largest diagonal contribution to the squared projection error of the field after the step. Evaluation of a search subspace should measure how accurately it represents the field change and the local response.

99. 【2611.25819】SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819

作者：Nico Leuze,Samed Dogan,Maximilian Hoh,Henry Schaub,Alfred Schöttl

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： to appear in Proceedings of the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV), 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Industrial bin picking requires pose estimates that are accurate under clutter, partial occlusion, and the reflective, texture-less nature of manufactured parts. Most recent approaches to 6D pose estimation follow multi-stage pipelines. These achieve high accuracy, but incur memory and runtime footprints that do not cope with the operational constraints of many real-world industrial deployments. Holistic pipelines estimate poses in a single pass, with cost controlled through indiscriminate subsampling or lowered resolution. Continuing this line, we present SCCV-6D, a fully sparse multi-view RGB-D estimator. We reconstruct the 3D space at millimeter precision, prune background via a staged heatmap mechanism, and predict class, translation offset and rotation at highest resolution for an arbitrary number of objects simultaneously. Rotation is formulated as canonical coordinate voting. Points vote for their canonical object coordinates with an associated confidence, enabling closed-form pose recovery with a symmetry-aware weighted Procrustes solve. Along an in-cluster point recovery induced by offset votes, this removes time-consuming post-processing stages. We reuse the task-supervised attention priors from the heatmap formulation for a novel attention-gated distillation objective that reweights teacher-student feature alignment. On the IPD test split we report competitive 0.826 AP at 1.13 s per scene, significantly faster than the leading entries. On MV-YCB our approach reaches 96.1 ADD-S, and remains competitive even on the densely packed bins of ROBI.

100. 【2611.25815】What Places an Action Boundary? Structure-Aware Attribution for Temporal Action Localization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25815

作者：Ruoshan Lan,Zongru Shao,Chieh Tsai,Mihai Surdeanu

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Temporal action localization (TAL) detectors must identify actions and estimate their temporal boundaries, but localization remains a major source of detection errors. While understanding what determines boundary placement requires faithful attribution of temporal evidence, existing attribution methods have not sufficiently addressed the temporal structure of TAL or the need to tailor attribution to boundary localization. We introduce a structure-aware attribution framework that averages contributions across random evidence-restoration orders following the Shapley-Shubik principle.
