160. 【2611.25458】RemoteVLPG: How to Obtain a Specific VLM for Language-Guided Path Generation on Remote Sensing Images?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25458

作者：Junzhe Dang,Mengmeng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Wei Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：task-specific Vision-Language Model, remote sensing images, Vision-Language Model, sensing images, obtain a task-specific

备注： 18 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We study how to obtain a task-specific Vision-Language Model (VLM) for language-guided path generation between start and end points on remote sensing images. We explore this question from three aspects. First, we implement three widely used architectural approaches and compare their performance. Second, we design a scalable method for constructing pre-training data and build an effective pre-training dataset with a degree of diversity. Methods trained on this dataset achieve promising zero-shot performance. Third, we construct three benchmarks that support stratified evaluation and design three metrics for path generation. We investigate this problem through extensive experiments. The results show that the zero-shot performance of methods trained on the constructed pre-training dataset can exceed that of some advanced commercial VLMs. Code and Dataset can be available at this https URL

161. 【2611.25457】CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25457

作者：Junhyeok Lee,Byung-Hoon Kim,Kyu Sung Choi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：reinforcement learning, learning usually relies, auxiliary evaluators, Post-training, reward hacking

备注： 10 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Post-training text-to-image generators with reinforcement learning usually relies on external reward models that need annotated preferences or auxiliary evaluators and are prone to reward hacking. We show that the generator's own denoising accuracy, used by a recent intrinsic reward, is a poor proxy for prompt fidelity. Within a same-prompt group, this absolute self-confidence is dominated by image complexity and ranks correct samples above incorrect ones near chance. We introduce CANDOR (Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio), the log-ratio of unconditional to conditional denoising errors under shared noise probes, which measures how much the prompt helps the model denoise its own sample. It cancels prompt-independent difficulty that scales both errors and ranks the better of two same-prompt samples higher in 60% and 63% of pairs on compositional generation and text rendering. As the only Flow-GRPO reward for a flow-matching model, CANDOR improves text rendering, aesthetic and human-preference scores over the base model and a prior absolute self-confidence reward, with text-rendering and preference gains that stronger classifier-free guidance does not reproduce. On public Flow-GRPO adapters trained on external rewards, CANDOR further raises PickScore and ImageReward, so it can complement those rewards. Code is available at this https URL.

162. 【2611.25454】Lossless Latent Diffusion Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25454

作者：Tongda Xu,Yu Zhang,Jun Hu,Huijuan Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：preserving pixel-exact reconstruction, pixel-exact reconstruction, preserving pixel-exact, diffusion models, diffusion

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We study the problem of training image diffusion models while preserving pixel-exact reconstruction. Prior works typically address this problem by directly modeling images in pixel space, requiring modifications to the diffusion training objective or model architecture. In this work, we introduce a different paradigm: rather than modifying diffusion models, we construct a lossless transformation that maps pixels into a representation more amenable to diffusion modeling. We term our approach the Lossless Latent Diffusion Model (LLDM). We construct LLDM using residual latent transformation and uneven latent scaling, and explain its effectiveness through a proposed metric, semantic rank priority (SRP). Empirically, LLDM substantially outperforms prior pixel-space diffusion models in generation quality and even surpasses several widely used lossy VAEs. Furthermore, we show that SRP is strongly correlated with downstream generative performance.

163. 【2611.25453】Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25453

作者：Hao Zhang,Yumin Huang,Linfeng Tang,Junjun Jiang,Jiayi Ma

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：forcing existing deep, simultaneously resemble multiple, resemble multiple source, lacks ground-truth fused, multiple source images

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image fusion often lacks ground-truth fused images, forcing existing deep methods to indirectly define fusion objectives by requiring the fused image to simultaneously resemble multiple source images. However, the distinct imaging characteristics of different modalities can impose competing constraints on the fused result, potentially limiting the effective integration of their complementary information. To address this long-standing challenge facing the image fusion community, we propose ApexFuse, a novel generative image fusion paradigm that moves beyond source-resemblance constraints toward learning a general distribution of high-quality scene representations. Specifically, we develop a human-LMM-in-the-loop pipeline that leverages large multimodal generative models and iterative human feedback to translate abstract fusion objectives into reliable visual references, thereby defining the target distribution of ideal fusion outcomes. Through target-distribution-guided adaptation, we transfer the rich generative priors of pretrained FLUX to image fusion, enabling high-quality scene reconstruction conditioned on multimodal observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ApexFuse harmoniously integrates salient thermal targets, natural colors, and fine textures, achieving superior visual quality as well as semantic segmentation and object detection performance over previous source-resemblance methods. Moreover, ApexFuse exhibits remarkable cross-task generalization, extending from infrared-visible fusion to multi-exposure fusion, multi-focus fusion, and visible-depth fusion without additional fine-tuning.

164. 【2611.25445】Stereo Visual Odometry with In-the-Loop Geometric Feed-Forward Models Towards Dense Metric Mapping

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25445

作者：Fahira Afzal Maken,Sagun Man Singh Shrestha,Edward Vanderfeen,Lucas Carvalho de Lima,Rongxin Li,Changming Sun,Milad Ramezani

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：geometric feed-forward models, integrates geometric feed-forward, stereo visual odometry, dense metric mapping, dense GFM predictions

备注： 8 PAGES, 6 FIGURES

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present a stereo visual odometry (SVO) framework that integrates geometric feed-forward models (GFMs) for dense depth prediction with factor-graph-based visual odometry to achieve accurate localisation and dense metric mapping. Our key idea is to exploit sparse but reliable metric geometry estimated from stereo and temporal multi-view observations to calibrate dense GFM predictions, while retaining the robustness and efficiency of model-based SVO. Rather than directly conditioning the GFM on potentially noisy stereo depth, optimised 3D landmarks are used to jointly estimate a scale-shift transformation over all keyframes within each local submap, yielding metrically consistent dense depth predictions. To balance accuracy and computational efficiency, we introduce a hierarchical factor-graph architecture. At the local level, sliding-window optimisation estimates high-frequency camera poses and multi-view-refined 3D landmarks. Dense predictions are generated only for selected keyframes and registered within each submap using SE(3) Generalised ICP. Consecutive submaps are further aligned in Sim(3) using their shared geometry, allowing residual scale inconsistencies to be corrected. These intra- and inter-submap constraints are incorporated into a global factor graph to produce a consistent trajectory and dense metric reconstruction. Extensive experiments on public and in-house datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive odometry accuracy against geometric and learning-based baselines while providing accurate dense metric reconstruction and improved robustness to failures in learned depth prediction.

165. 【2611.25444】VisualTalk-Bench: Evaluating Question-Driven Avatar-Led Video Explanations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25444

作者：Xin Zhang,Tujie Xu,Zhiyi Chen,Xiaodong Zhang,Sicheng Yang,Minglei Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：answer user questions, avatar synthesis enable, synthesis enable, user questions, answer user

备注： 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Advances in video generation and avatar synthesis enable new ways to answer user questions through visual explanations. This task requires a system to formulate a correct answer, translate relevant information into visual material, and deliver the explanation through an avatar's speech and actions. Existing benchmarks rarely assess whether avatars point to the visual content being discussed. We introduce VisualTalk-Bench for question-driven avatar-led video explanations in finance and Chinese and English education. It includes 800 curated reference videos and 100 independent evaluation requests, assessing Content Factuality, Speech-Visual Consistency, Visual Quality, Motion Smoothness, and Avatar-Content Pointing. Yet end-to-end video generators often struggle to align gestures with specific visual targets. To explore this challenge, we develop Avatar-O Agent, a modular reference implementation with explicit interaction planning. Experiments across six agents show that factual precision and visual quality do not guarantee accurate pointing. A 26-participant study comparing four agents finds that Avatar-O Agent receives the highest average ratings for visual guidance, perceived pointing accuracy, and explanation effectiveness. We will release the data and code.

166. 【2611.25437】Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437

作者：Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Xuelian Shi,Xiang Chen,Dehe Zhao,Banglong Nong,Wenhao Zheng,Lei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：semantic associations make, daily necessities, targeted semantic attacks, naturally on clothing, clothing and daily

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Logos appear naturally on clothing and daily necessities, where their recognizable shapes and semantic associations make them familiar visual cues. This familiarity helps them blend into real-world environments and remain visually inconspicuous. We ask whether an existing logo can serve as a carrier for coordinated targeted semantic attacks on visible-infrared (VIS-IR) vision-language models (VLMs) without changing its original outline. This setting requires expressive adversarial variation within a fixed shape while accounting for different appearance characteristics and model responses across modalities. We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo's interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. LoSA uses a contour field and low-frequency graph spectral modes to structure the interior color layout, a frozen appearance adapter to couple VIS and IR appearances, and particle swarm optimization to jointly optimize shared design parameters. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that LoSA consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines in simultaneous Top-1 targeted success. Cross-architecture experiments, visual naturalness ratings, and captioning/VQA transfer analyses further characterize its effectiveness and limitations. These findings reveal that familiar visual symbols from real-world environments can serve as shape-preserving carriers for coordinated targeted semantic attacks across imaging modalities.

167. 【2611.25436】Raptor: Rejection-aware Adaptive Tree based on Speculative Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25436

作者：Qishu Hu,Haozhao Wang,Zicong Hong,Yufeng Zhan,Yuanqing Xia

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：accelerates vision-language models, verifying multiple candidate, multiple candidate continuations, Tree-structured speculative decoding, Tree-structured speculative

备注： 18pages, 7figures, 3tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tree-structured speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models (VLMs) by verifying multiple candidate continuations in parallel. However, long visual contexts increase the cost of speculative computation, making candidate coverage and redundant expansion important considerations beyond tree topology adaptation. We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

168. 【2611.25419】DiLE: Discrimination-Aware Latent-Space Learning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25419

作者：Ben Kang,Junda Wang,Yuxuan Yuan,Hang Li,Yuhan Liu,Xin Chen,Wanting Geng,Tong Lin,Pei Fu,Dong Wang,Jian Luan,Huchuan Lu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Multimodal large language, greatly improved input, universal multimodal embedding, improved input understanding, Multimodal large

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal large language models have greatly improved input understanding in universal multimodal embedding models. However, this understanding does not ensure that their embeddings preserve the evidence needed to distinguish relevant items from confusable alternatives. To preserve essential discriminative evidence in embeddings, this paper proposes DiLE, a discrimination-aware latent-space learning framework that combines task guidance with on-policy retrieval feedback. Specifically, a few learnable queries are used to encode task-relevant information in a compact latent space. Reasoning instructions and auxiliary answer-generation supervision shape these representations to support both task understanding and retrieval discrimination. To address task-specific discriminative needs, we design shared-private expert adaptation that integrates cross-task knowledge sharing with task-specific representation learning. Building on this, the on-policy hard-negative distillation is introduced: the student retrieves confusable candidates, and a reverse KL divergence objective aligns its embedding-based similarity distribution with the teacher's fine-grained relevance distribution over these candidates. Candidates are periodically refreshed using updated student snapshots, tying teacher supervision to the student's evolving retrieval behavior and confusions. Across four model configurations that span two backbone generations, DiLE achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMEB-v2, UVRB, and MRMR, demonstrating its effectiveness across modalities and tasks.

169. 【2611.25418】Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning for Mammography Vision--Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25418

作者：Zhengbo Zhou,Dooman Arefan,Shandong Wu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：redundant visual information, extensive normal anatomy, Medical vision, identify small, visual information

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical vision--language models (VLMs) often need to identify small, subtle lesions within high-resolution images containing extensive normal anatomy and redundant visual information. Visual-token compression improves efficiency, but preserving salient regions alone can discard the context needed for diagnostic interpretation. This context can extend across images: in bilateral mammography, the opposite breast provides a patient-specific anatomical reference. Yet retaining such evidence does not ensure that the model uses it effectively. We propose Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning (BESR), which connects evidence preservation with comparative assessment through a bilateral-guided token selector and a bilateral reward. Under a shared token budget, the selector jointly prioritizes lesion appearance and its local and bilateral context, while the reward encourages assessments that benefit from the preserved comparison. Experiments on a bilateral dataset constructed from CBIS-DDSM and on VinDr-Mammo demonstrate improved evidence retention and diagnostic performance under constrained visual-token budgets. Inference benchmarking further shows reduced prefill latency.

170. 【2611.25417】MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417

作者：Xinzhe Fu,Qi Shao,Yuanfei Xie,Taoyu Wu,Zhihao Cao,Long Hu,Hong Liang,Xianzhi Li,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang,Rongsheng Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：single RGB-D image, Recovering accurate visible-surface, RGB-D image, single RGB-D, object mask

备注： 9 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recovering accurate visible-surface depth from a single RGB-D image and an object mask is essential for robotic grasping and manipulation of transparent objects. Specular reflections cause missing depth, while refraction corrupts nonzero returns with background depth, making raw measurements unreliable as surface anchors. Under a fixed sampling budget, refining an initial single-view prediction within a single search interval either misses large refraction offsets or sacrifices fine local resolution. To resolve this single-view trade-off, MIR-Depth constructs Coordinate-Aware Multi-Range Hypotheses: a 3D-aggregated global inverse-depth volume and depth-axis-filtered pixel-wise local volumes around a prior-guided initial depth. Selective Recurrent Refinement then introduces out-of-bounds lookup masking and validity-masked softmax fusion to iteratively update inverse depth from these volumes using a multi-scale ConvGRU. Finally, Spatially Gated Correction applies a learned per-pixel spatial gate to the full-resolution residual, preserving accurate initial geometry without imposing raw sensor readings as hard constraints. Experiments on TransCG, TODD, TRansPose, ClearPose, and HouseCat6D show that MIR-Depth achieves lower error than ReMake, DFNet, BP-Net, and PSD. Real-robot evaluations confirm accurate surface geometry for 6-DoF grasp pose prediction. Code and pretrained models will be publicly available.

171. 【2611.25414】Watertight Mesh Optimization of Textured Objects

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25414

作者：Nathaniel Burgdorfer,Philippos Mordohai

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：reconstructing high-quality, existing approaches, reconstructed surfaces, approaches often excel, objective while compromising

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:When reconstructing high-quality 3D assets from images, existing approaches often excel at one objective while compromising another, and frequently overlook the intrinsic quality of the reconstructed surfaces. We present MODL, a framework that jointly optimizes geometry and view-dependent appearance directly on a watertight triangle mesh. Our approach combines Laplacian-preconditioned optimization for smooth vertex updates with curvature-adaptive remeshing to preserve fine details and well-shaped triangles. The triangle mesh is optimized subject to multi-view normalized cross-correlation constraints, but without segmentation, geometric supervision or alpha-blending. Evaluations on object reconstruction datasets demonstrate that MODL achieves competitive novel-view synthesis while producing geometrically accurate models with superior intrinsic mesh quality. Our results highlight the potential of direct mesh optimization to unify photorealistic rendering, geometric reconstruction, and practical 3D asset creation.

172. 【2611.25408】FG-GraphBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Fine-Grained Visual Understanding with Multimodal Scene Graphs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25408

作者：Sicheng Liu,Xunkai Li,Ziyu Han,Xingnan Zhang,Ru Zhang,Rong-Hua Li

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：large language models, recognize small visual, requires multimodal large, multimodal large language, small visual entities

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Fine-grained visual understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to recognize small visual entities and understand their relationships. Scene graphs offer a natural way to represent this information, organizing visual entities as nodes and their relationships as edges. However, we identify two challenges: existing scene graph benchmarks provide insufficient coverage of fine-grained entities and the relationships among them, and existing methods for fine-grained visual understanding have yet to effectively leverage such graph-structured information in MLLMs. To address these challenges, we introduce Fine-Grained Multimodal Scene Graph Benchmark (FG-GraphBench) and FiGRA. FG-GraphBench is a benchmark comprising three complementary tasks: fine-grained visual question answering (FG-QA), image captioning (FG-Caption), and image description generation (FG-Description). We develop a two-stage construction pipeline that discovers and grounds fine-grained entities and establishes their relations to construct the fine-grained multimodal scene graphs (MSGs) in FG-GraphBench. Building on these MSGs, we propose FiGRA, a Fine-grained Graph Representation Augmentation method for MLLMs. FiGRA adopts a two-stage training strategy. It encodes multimodal entity and relation features as continuous graph tokens, and integrates them into the MLLM. Experiments show that FiGRA significantly outperforms strong baselines across three tasks of FG-GraphBench. Further analyses confirm the effectiveness of FG-GraphBench and highlight the importance of preserving fine-grained entity and relation information for visual understanding.

173. 【2611.25406】Decision Boundaries Matter: Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation for Cross-Domain Fake Image Detection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25406

作者：Shichuang Xie,Qishuai Ding,Tong Qiao,Liquan Shen,Guorui Feng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：mismatched decision boundaries, fake image detectors, fake image, due to mismatched, learning transferable features

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Despite learning transferable features, fake image detectors may struggle on unseen domains due to mismatched decision boundaries. Our analysis shows that features extracted by the frozen forensic encoder remain discriminative, but the source-trained decision boundary does not fully exploit this information. However, correcting this boundary without source data or target labels remains challenging, as unreliable source predictions may lead to incorrect adaptation. To address this issue, we propose Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation (GraRA), which refines the source decision boundary using only unlabeled target data. To determine which classifier directions to update, GraRA constructs a compact residual subspace from uncertainty-weighted target features and keeps it orthogonal to the source classification direction. To guide these updates, it uses source-score anchors to generate soft labels through target-graph diffusion, while neighborhood consistency regularizes residual learning within the constrained subspace. The learned correction is merged into the source classifier, requiring only 32 optimized coefficients and no additional inference branches. On diverse unseen OpenMMSec domains, GraRA achieves 84.36\% accuracy and 91.34\% AUC, improving over the source detector by 4.33 and 3.50 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis shows that reliable source predictions and locally consistent neighborhoods help guide residual learning, allowing GraRA to correct ranking errors that a simple threshold shift cannot address. \textcolor{cvprblue}{Code will be made publicly available.

174. 【2611.25391】HOPD: Historical On-Policy Distillation for SAR MLLM Continual Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25391

作者：Yuxuan Li,Derrick Goh Xin Deik,Zheng-Peng Duan,Qianru Sun,Weijie Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multimodal large language, large language models, interpretation requires multimodal, requires multimodal large, learn incoming sources

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Continual SAR interpretation requires multimodal large language models to learn incoming sources while retaining earlier task abilities. This is difficult when acquisition and geographic shifts alter visual evidence, regionally concentrated categories bias answers, and historical images are scarce. We address this problem with Historical On-Policy Distillation (HOPD), which reuses source-acquisition LoRA states as persistent teachers for one evolving student. Archiving each state before consolidation preserves a stable source reference. The teachers supervise student-generated prefixes, transferring historical behavior to the student's changing generation contexts. To enrich sparse replay, original-view teachers guide augmented historical student views while preserving spatial coordinates. This knowledge is consolidated into a single deployed adapter that requires no source identity. On SARVQA-CL, our source-incremental evaluation protocol, HOPD achieves the highest final mean score of 54.51 among the compared methods, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.96 points. It also improves the final source-macro score in every evaluated task over shared-memory Replay-SFT, with gains across source orders. These results support source-acquisition history as supervision for retaining earlier task performance during continual SAR adaptation. Code and data: this https URL.

175. 【2611.25389】Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25389

作者：Rui Hong,Xugang Ye,Jana Košecká,Kai Fan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multi-shot video generation, shared scene backgrounds, existing panorama generators, single input image, video generation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Panoramas can serve as shared scene backgrounds for multi-shot video generation, but most existing panorama generators take text alone or a single input image. We present a single model that generates a full equirectangular panorama from a text caption and K in {0,...,4} perspective conditioning views taken by a four-camera rig at one viewpoint. We fine-tune a pretrained text-to-image rectified-flow transformer in two stages. In the first stage, each conditioning view is appended to the token sequence together with the ID of its camera slot, and the number of views varies during training, so one set of weights serves every input setting. In the second stage, we improve the region not covered by any conditioning view. Supervised training computes its loss on noisy latents of the ground truth and never scores a panorama the model has generated and decoded, so we propose Uncovered-Region GRPO (UR-GRPO), a reinforcement-learning recipe that rewards the candidates a vision-language model prefers in pairwise comparisons on crops around this region. Because the slot ID alone does not tell the model which way the rig points, we also offer an optional variant that writes each view's camera direction into its position encoding, which lets the user decide where each view appears in the output. With one conditioning view, our model outperforms the previous state of the art, 360Anything, trained on the same data and evaluated with the same code, on Laval Indoor and gives mixed results on an indoor subset of SUN360; the second stage improves the results for every number of conditioning views.

176. 【2611.25382】xt Lives in the Guidance: Preventing Silent Text Rewriting in Cached Diffusion Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25382

作者：Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Weijia Jia

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diffusion transformers, text, Diffusion, feature caching, legible text

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion transformers (DiTs) now render long, legible text in images, and feature caching is a standard way to accelerate them. Caches are judged by image-level metrics, which do not check whether the rendered text is still correct. We find that caching silently rewrites this text. On Z-Image, TaylorSeer at 2.9x fewer FLOPs lowers word accuracy from 62% to 46%, while ImageReward and CLIP Score barely change. Sampling with fewer steps at the same compute keeps almost all of this text, so the loss comes from how the cache predicts the skipped steps. Decomposing the cache error along classifier-free guidance (CFG), we trace this loss mainly to the guidance term, the difference between the conditional and unconditional predictions, which caches extrapolate worst on text. Correcting the guidance inside the text alone recovers most of the lost text. We therefore propose GuideCache, which recomputes both CFG branches only on the tokens with the largest predicted guidance error, using the guidance terms that the cache already stores. At 2.4x fewer FLOPs, GuideCache restores full-compute prompt fidelity on Z-Image and Qwen-Image, and on Z-Image it renders text as well as or better than ToCa, TeaCache, and FasterCache while staying much closer to the full model's output. GuideCache works with caches of any order, and on top of fewer sampling steps it keeps more text than step reduction at a higher speedup. These results suggest that acceleration methods for text-rendering models should be evaluated on the text they render.

177. 【2611.25371】A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25371

作者：Songlin Li,Hui Ma,Zhiqing Guo,Dan Ma,Gaobo Yang,Xin Liao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Existing weakly supervised, weakly supervised image, annotation-specific training strategies, Existing weakly, image manipulation localization

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing weakly supervised image manipulation localization (IML) methods typically rely on annotation-specific training strategies, hindering the joint use of diverse annotations within a single model. This limits the scale of usable training data and constrains further progress. We propose UniIML, a teacher-student framework that maps diverse weak annotations into a unified representation to guide the Segment Anything Model (SAM) in generating multiple candidate masks. However, SAM is designed for general-purpose segmentation and has limited sensitivity to manipulation traces; its predicted quality scores alone may therefore fail to reliably identify the candidate that best delineates the manipulated region. We introduce Ordinal Safe Consensus (OSC), which first filters candidates using trusted weak-annotation constraints encoded in the unified representation. It then ranks the remaining candidates separately by consistency with weak annotations, SAM-predicted quality scores, and cross-view stability, aggregating these rankings for an initial selection. To assess how changes in the evaluation criteria affect candidate selection, OSC repeats ranking and selection while omitting one criterion at a time. The most frequently selected candidate is adopted as the final mask, and its relative selection frequency quantifies selection stability. We construct soft pseudo-labels from the selected candidate's predicted probabilities and estimate pixel-level supervision weights based on selection stability, pixel-level agreement among candidates, and predictive certainty. The student receives direct supervision in regions defined by weak annotations and learns from soft pseudo-labels elsewhere through reliability-weighted distillation, enabling unified training across weak annotation types. Experiments show that UniIML achieves superior average performance compared with state-of-the-art IML methods.

178. 【2611.25369】Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25369

作者：Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Yanzeng Li,Weijia Jia

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Looped vision-language models, repeatedly applying weight-tied, Looped vision-language, deepen visual reasoning, applying weight-tied modules

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen visual reasoning by repeatedly applying weight-tied modules. However, their decoding memory grows with recurrent depth and limits the image size and batch size that fit in a fixed memory budget. Each recurrent step produces its own key-value (KV) states, so the cache holds several copies of the same image. We find that, after an exact prefill, earlier steps still need access to visual information but can reuse the final visual KV within the same module. We propose FoldCache, which compresses visual KV along the recurrent axis while preserving the full prefill computation. FoldCache partitions recurrent steps within each module into groups and retains the final visual KV of each group for its members to share during decoding. Sharing stays within module boundaries, while question and answer KV remain separate for each step. The grouping granularity controls the degree of compression, and folding can be combined with token eviction and quantization to further reduce memory use. On LoopVL, FoldCache approximately halves prefix KV memory while matching full-cache accuracy on five open-ended benchmarks. These memory savings support batch sizes up to 1.7 times those of a full cache under the same GPU memory budget, and up to 2.6 times with the more aggressive FoldCache-Lite.

179. 【2611.25347】Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347

作者：Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Chenhao Wu,Zixiang Ni,Yingguang Yang,Feng Zhang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Visual world models, JEPA-WM plan, Visual world, DINO-WM and JEPA-WM, cost

备注： 43 pages, 10 figures, 30 tables; supplementary material included

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual world models such as DINO-WM and JEPA-WM plan with an optimizer that searches for actions whose predicted outcome has the lowest cost, its distance to a goal image. A plan can thus fail in two ways: the optimizer never tries a good action that the cost would prefer (a search failure), or the cost, computed on predictions, ranks a bad action first (a scoring failure). The two need different remedies, more search or a better model, but a planner does not record which one occurred. We tell them apart by executing the optimizer's candidates, scoring a known solution with the planner's own cost, and comparing costs on predicted and real outcomes. An error model explains when each arises and so how to use retrieved demonstration windows (DemoProp): start the search from them (a warm start) when prediction errors are small, and average them without the model when large errors make the optimizer pick overrated actions. Across four simulated environments and three world models, on short tasks whose goals the planner can represent, PushT and Wall fail mostly by search, while Metaworld and RoboCasa fail by scoring because the cost, sound on real outcomes (AUROC 0.94 on Metaworld), is at chance on predictions (0.54). The diagnosis tells which remedy pays off: a four times larger search lowers the real-outcome cost only where failures come from search (clearly for DINO-WM), and where they come from scoring, averaging the windows without the model solves 127 of 168 new Metaworld tasks, against at most 87 when the model chooses among them. Within PushT, degrading only the model flips which use is better, and our rule, frozen in advance, follows the flip and solves 678 of 960 new episodes, against 530 for the best fixed use.

Abstract:Reconstructing an editable 3D scene from video requires matching visible structure while plausibly completing unseen surfaces. Agents can write scene-construction programs, but direct reconstruction attempts often produce errors in layout, scale, and lighting. We introduce SceneAnything, an automated system for reconstructing mesh scenes from real and generated videos, enabling text-to-scene construction when paired with video generation. A software harness coordinates agents, pretrained vision models, and graphics tools through an explicit reconstruction protocol. Shared camera, depth, and segmentation estimates ground component construction: a scene-owner agent plans the environment and briefs authors, who write parameterized Blender programs. A visibility- and fit-based level-of-detail rule selects the construction level, while unseen regions are completed and marked as inferred. 181. 【2611.25338】VideoRNG: Relightable Neural Gaussians from Generative Video Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25338

作者：Bowen Xue,Saeed Hadadan,Fabrice Rousselle,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Zahra Montazeri,Miloš Hašan

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generating realistic 3D assets remains constrained by classical representations: surface meshes and compact physically based materials struggle to express objects whose appearance arises from fine geometry or complex light transport, while existing Gaussian-splatting generators typically bake fixed illumination into appearance. We introduce VideoRNG, a two-stage method that generates complete, relightable 3D assets from a text prompt or a single image without prescribing a mesh or analytic material model. A video generator fine-tuned from a pretrained video diffusion backbone first produces a structured RGBA turntable sequence under a known pose, camera, and illumination. A feed-forward large reconstruction model initialized from a pretrained video diffusion backbone then maps the video-VAE latent frames of selected turntable frames directly to millions of anisotropic Gaussians with learned neural appearance features. A shared directional decoder and stochastic Gaussian ray tracer turn this representation, which we call ray-traced Relightable Neural Gaussians (rtRNG), into radiance under novel viewpoints and illumination. To preserve high-frequency appearance while learning geometry, we introduce depth-anchored appearance training, and we make million-Gaussian reconstruction practical through foreground token selection and cost-aware distributed sampling. VideoRNG generates complex assets such as fur, feathers, and spider webs that can be interactively rendered, relit, and integrated into scenes.

184. 【2611.25297】ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25297

作者：Chen Liu,Zhen Fang,Xin Yu,Dadong Wang,Ling Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：intended interface element, GUI grounding maps, natural language instruction, GUI grounding, interface element

备注： Accepted by ICDM@D2AI 2026 Best Paper Award

点击查看摘要

Abstract:GUI grounding maps a screenshot and a natural language instruction to the coordinates of the intended interface element. Existing approaches improve grounding through visual scaling, instruction scaling, and sampling scaling, yet it remains unclear which grounding bottlenecks these strategies address and where their gains saturate. To address this gap, we introduce ScaleDiag, a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates the effects of visual input, instruction information, and prediction sampling on GUI grounding by independently varying target scale and surrounding context, target-specific information and auxiliary descriptions, and candidate coverage and selection, respectively. We evaluate ScaleDiag across five benchmarks and up to eight vision-language models and find that each scaling strategy operates within a much narrower regime than commonly assumed. (1) Visual scaling is governed by target occupancy rather than a fixed crop ratio, with full-screen errors typically rescued once the target occupies only 0.32% of the cropped view, while further zooming eventually becomes harmful. (2) Instruction scaling is driven by information that identifies the target rather than description richness itself, as even ground-truth-derived attributes such as color, size, and shape are often neutral or harmful, while appearance and spatial descriptions account for over 90% of the achievable gain. (3) Repeated sampling exposes a different limitation, as correct coordinates are frequently generated but remain difficult to select, leaving 5.1--11.0 points of oracle headroom while geometric consensus provides almost no improvement. Together, these results show that test-time scaling is constrained by distinct operating regimes rather than benefiting monotonically from more visual focus, richer instructions, or additional samples. Our code is available at this https URL.

185. 【2611.25296】CalibCache: Cache-to-Full Feature Dynamics Calibration for Diffusion Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25296

作者：Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu,Jingwei Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diffusion Transformers generate, Transformers generate high-quality, Diffusion Transformers, generate high-quality images, remains computationally expensive

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images, but iterative sampling remains computationally expensive. Feature caching reduces the number of full network evaluations, yet its approximation error can become more severe under short sampling schedules, where fewer full-compute steps and a coarser sampling grid can make history-based extrapolation less reliable. In this paper, we propose CalibCache, a lightweight offline calibration method that corrects systematic discrepancies between cached and full-compute feature dynamics. Specifically, CalibCache calibrates feature acceleration, defined as the second-order finite difference across sampling steps. Using only a single pair of full-compute and naive-cache trajectories at each setting, CalibCache fits scalar acceleration maps offline by centered least squares. These maps are then applied during sampling without additional full-network evaluations or online optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image on DrawBench and DiT-XL/2 on ImageNet-256, CalibCache consistently improves fidelity or generation quality over TaylorSeer under 20-step sampling at cache intervals $N=3,4,5$. At $N=5$, it reduces FLUX.1-dev LPIPS from $0.563$ to $0.429$ while achieving a $2.86\times$ denoising speedup over full-compute sampling, and lowers DiT-XL/2 FID from $16.39$ to $10.37$ relative to TaylorSeer.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dense metric mapping from a single camera and IMU remains challenging: visual-inertial odometry (VIO) provides metric motion but sparse geometry, whereas feed-forward depth models predict dense structure without ensuring metric scale. We present Accord, which constructs an accurate online dense metric map from any camera with an IMU. A VIO filter publishes metric poses, a depth foundation model conditioned on them predicts depth for short keyframe windows, and a factor graph fuses the resulting submaps. Recent work has explored several approaches to this problem. What is essential is the multi-view agreement (Accord), which is considered in SLAM as a source of geometric self-consistency. We evaluate a single mapping configuration across five public camera-IMU setups and a new Phone-rig/GoPro dataset. 188. 【2611.25265】Real Cameras are Radiometric

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25265

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 22 pages, including supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We propose the radiometric camera, a model that lifts any central geometric camera model to a radiometric one. The lifted model keeps the projection of the geometric model and adds a finite aperture, a free-form focus surface and a generic vignetting function. We calibrate it by inverse rendering directly on the relative irradiance of the sensor, from the same images of standard calibration targets used in geometric calibration. Geometric calibration absorbs the bias of its feature detections. The proposed radiometric calibration uses no features, and our radiometric cameras have lower reprojection errors on held-out test sets. The focus surface extends defocus modeling from the thin lens to any central camera, and we use it in a first multi-view radiometric reconstruction, which recovers the in-focus reflectance of a texture from defocused images. The vignetting is estimated jointly and accurately reproduces a flat field measured through a diffuser. Because every standard camera model lifts to a radiometric one, the radiometric camera naturally and directly extends generative scene models beyond multi-view geometry to multi-view radiometric ones, where phenomena such as lighting and attenuation can be accurately estimated.

189. 【2611.25257】Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25257

作者：Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Qinglong Zheng,Ziwen Wang,Chaoran Cheng,Changyi Yang,Hanqi Mao,Ge Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：weights, frontier model repair, Small reasoning VLMs, vision-language model, VLM

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Can a frontier model repair a vision-language model (VLM) by rewriting its weights? Small reasoning VLMs such as Qwen3.5 need such a repair: in thinking mode they fall into loops on many image questions, repeating themselves until the token limit, spending the whole budget and losing the answer. The usual remedies act on the decoding, leaving the looping model as it was, or must be told what to change. Yet the cause is written in the weights. Keel, the first agentic weight-editing method for VLMs on image tasks, finds and removes it there. Told only the symptom, an editor, a frontier multimodal LLM acting as an agent, locates the cause, edits the weights and tests them, with a failure census and instruments showing it the failing images and where the VLM attends; among several editors, the harness keeps only edits that clearly reduce the failure without lowering accuracy on questions no editor saw. The repair needs no training, keeps the model's size and per-token cost, and applies to already-edited weights. The editors expose where the loops live: from the original weights, independent editors on both models trace them to the attention outputs of the middle layers, and the smaller VLM stops consulting the image inside a loop. On Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-2B, across six image benchmarks from visual mathematics to GUI grounding, the edit removes most loops on every benchmark and shortens answers, and the edited models are significantly more accurate than the thinking models on average; the larger one gains most on GUI grounding, a task absent from the practice questions. Because the repair lives in the weights, it combines with budget forcing where held-out questions favor it: on the best available weights, Keel has the best average accuracy on both models, significantly above every training-free baseline we compare on the larger one.

191. 【2611.25239】Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25239

作者：Rihan Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Yuhan Chen,Sina Farsiu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：requires distinguishing task-relevant, distinguishing task-relevant content, degradation requires distinguishing, concealed object segmentation, Reliable visual perception

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reliable visual perception under degradation requires distinguishing task-relevant content from degradation-induced variations, especially in concealed object segmentation (COS), where foreground cues are inherently weak. The central challenge is not merely improving visibility, but identifying reliable content evidence amid degradation-induced variations. RGB-space segmentation often entangles content and degradation cues, while restoration-based methods primarily focus on image recovery and task interaction. This motivates explicit factorization to distinguish content evidence from degradation effects and assess its reliability. Retinex provides a spatially aligned basis for this separation but remains illumination-centric. We therefore extend it to content-degradation formation with task-asymmetric factor roles. The Factor Unfolding Network (FUN) uses the content factor R as the sole source of direct semantic evidence for segmentation, while the degradation factor L captures degradation-related variations and generates a spatial reliability map to calibrate this evidence. A unified energy couples observation formation with reliability-weighted foreground-background separation, and its alternating updates yield a coarse-to-fine network without clean-image supervision. In this way, FUN factorizes degradation for perception rather than removing it. Extensive experiments across clean and degraded conditions and diverse segmentation tasks demonstrate robust performance and generalization. Code will be released.

192. 【2611.25228】MKA: Evaluating Multimodal Knowledge Agents on end-to-end professional tasks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25228

作者：Anima Singh,Suhas Yogin,Weicheng Kuo,Ivor Rendulic,Ivan Jurin,Antoine Yang,Oran Kelly,Rohan Doshi,Hammad Ayyubi,Ioana Bica,Sergi Caelles,Matthew Conniff,Dan Graur,Anastasija Ilić,Ahmet Iscen,Chao Jia,Tomasz Kępa,Chunyuan Li,Noah Ó Donnaile,Filip Pavetic,Goran Petrovic,Khalid Salama,Zhicheng Wang,Polina Zablotskaia,Yiqian Zhang,Ming Zhao,Benjamin Caine,Mario Lučić,Melvin Johnson,Radu Soricut,Jean-Baptiste Alayrac

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：professional knowledge work, autonomous agents perform, Multimodal Knowledge Agent, professional knowledge, knowledge work

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We introduce the Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) benchmark, which measures how well autonomous agents perform end-to-end professional knowledge work that depends on visual understanding. Each task requires an agent to extract and combine information from multiple visually dense files, such as CAD blueprints, electronics schematics, radiographs, complex charts and geospatial maps, and to produce a standalone deliverable in .xlsx, .pptx, .pdf, .docx, .html or .png format. Existing benchmarks leave this capability largely untested: removing image-viewing tools leaves Gemini 4 Carbon's score on GDPval unchanged, and single-format evaluations such as GDP$.$pdf and GDP$.$xlsx do not require reasoning across files or modalities. MKA contains 200 tasks across 10 economic sectors. Deliverables are graded against expert-weighted rubrics by a sandboxed agentic autorater that agrees with human domain experts on 96.2% of criterion judgments in our expert-graded study (Cohen's $\kappa = 0.91$), scored by weighted satisfaction rate (WSR) overall and on frontier criteria unsaturated by prior-generation models (Climb-WSR). Across the frontier models we evaluate (Gemini 4 Carbon, Claude Fable 5.2, Claude Opus 5.5, and GPT-6 Astra), Claude Fable 5.2 (56.6% Climb-WSR, 73.3% Overall WSR) and Gemini 4 Carbon (54.3% Climb-WSR, 71.9% Overall WSR) achieve the highest scores, while Carbon uses $5.7\times$ fewer tokens. Even for the strongest models, Climb-WSR remains below 57%, leaving substantial headroom for multimodal agents.

193. 【2611.25226】Reject and Report: Forgery-Evident Undetectable Watermarks for Generative Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25226

作者：Yihan Wang,Haochen Sun,Xi He,Yaoliang Yu,N. Asokan

类目：Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Provenance schemes, traced back, Provenance, generative model, watermarking scheme

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Provenance schemes such as those based on content watermarks let images from a generative model be traced back to it. A practical watermarking scheme must be accurate at a low false-positive rate (FPR), robust under content-preserving processing, and undetectable. In addition, it must resist forgery; otherwise, even from a single watermarked image and a surrogate model, an adversary can imprint the watermark onto an arbitrary image or regenerate new content that carries it, falsely attributing harmful content to a model or making authentic photographs appear AI-generated. We argue that a watermarking scheme should not only reject forgeries but also report them: a forgery rejected as unwatermarked leaves no trace of the attempt. State-of-the-art forgery-resilient undetectable watermarking schemes, such as SemBind, fall short on both counts. External anomaly detectors that do report forgeries either miss most of them or falsely flag genuine images after common processing. We propose Tripwire-WM, the first forgery-evident framework for undetectable watermarks. It commits the semantics of the generated image to the magnitudes of the initial latent noise, which the sign-based watermarks leave unused, through a distribution-preserving embedding; a forged image carries a valid watermark but with a commitment that does not match its content, so the detector both rejects it and reports it as forged. Built on the pseudorandom code (PRC) watermark and Gaussian Shading++, Tripwire-WM provably preserves their undetectability. On MS-COCO, with the owner's own model as the surrogate, it accepts no imprinting forgery, compared with up to 55.0% for SemBind, and reports all of them as forged; it also accepts at most 1.8% of reprompting forgeries, against up to 46.2% for SemBind. Meanwhile, it accepts at least 99% of genuine watermarked images at 10^{-6} FPR and matches the robustness of the base schemes.

194. 【2611.25215】Bridging vision-language reasoning and learned control for legged navigation in the wild

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25215

作者：Takahiro Miki,Robin Schmid,Manthan Patel,Turcan Tuna,Julia Richter,Julian Nubert,Joonho Lee,Matthias Müller,Marco Hutter

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Open-world Autonomous Mobility, Robust Open-world Autonomous, Autonomous Mobility, Open-world Autonomous, VLM

备注： 42 pages, 6 figures, 15 supplementary figures. Author's version of the article published in Science Robotics. Project page: [this https URL](https://roam-hiking.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autonomous navigation in diverse real-world environments remains one of the greatest challenges in robotics. It demands both robust low-level motion control to handle diverse terrain and high-level reasoning to interpret environmental cues. We present ROAM (Robust Open-world Autonomous Mobility), a system that enables legged robots to perform kilometer-scale navigation in complex alpine and urban settings. ROAM combines the world knowledge of a Vision-Language Model (VLM), pre-trained on internet-scale data, with robust locomotion and local planning policies learned in simulation via reinforcement learning (RL). The VLM provides common-sense reasoning and semantic interpretation of the scene, assessing risks, interpreting signs, and producing semantic labels with associated navigation costs. An open-vocabulary segmentation module converts these labels into a dense, real-time risk map, which conditions two pre-trained RL policies responsible for agile locomotion and local navigation, effectively coupling high-level VLM reasoning with low-level physical control. Real-world experiments showed the system's ability to autonomously navigate a wide range of complex real-world environments. The robot completes kilometer-long hikes with minimal intervention, interprets signs to avoid danger, and evaluates its environment to identify safe areas on which to step. This work represents a step toward the reliable deployment of systems in unstructured environments. However, it also underscores the challenges that remain in VLM reliability and spatial reasoning, indicating that foundational models are a crucial component but not yet a complete solution for navigation in the wild.

195. 【2611.25210】Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25210

作者：Dingqiang Ye,Jiacong Xu,Wen Zhang,Yiqun Mei,Vishal M. Patel

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：vision language models, effortless for humans, remains a bottleneck, bottleneck for vision, vision language

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tracking a hidden object through a video is effortless for humans, yet it remains a bottleneck for vision language models (VLMs). In the shell game, where a ball is hidden under one of several identical cups that are then swapped, VLMs that answer directly stay near chance, thinking models fail on long games, and fine-tuning learns only the lengths it has seen. We argue that the limit is serial computation: each swap must be composed with the last result, and a VLM of fixed depth has only a fixed number of state transitions for it. To address this, we propose RecurrentVLM, which makes every attention layer of a pretrained VLM recur over time by computing the keys and values of finished frame groups from the layer's own output. With a single answer token, RecurrentVLM reaches 100% on VET-Bench and 99.6% on MET-Bench, and 99% on 50-swap games after training on at most 30 swaps. We also observe several interesting phenomena: length generalization emerges suddenly at an aha moment; without recurrence, each swap costs an attention layer; the ball's state forms in the first four attention layers; and recurrence keeps the latest state at the cost of details of earlier moments. These results suggest that recurrent VLMs are a potential solution for tracking the states of unobserved objects in video. Code will be released.

196. 【2611.25204】Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25204

作者：Dingqiang Ye,Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Whole-body human recognition, Whole-body human, large vision models, advanced rapidly, rapidly with large

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Whole-body human recognition has advanced rapidly with large vision models. However, existing methods remain fragile under real-world variations, as they tie identity to transient cues such as clothing, texture, pose, or viewpoint. We argue that a robust recognizer should not merely learn to ignore these cues, but imagine how a person would be represented if they changed. To this end, we propose IDWorld, an identity world model: just as a latent world model predicts the features of a scene after an action, IDWorld predicts how a person would be represented under a changed condition. In a single forward pass, IDWorld imagines these counterfactual features under shared canonical conditions, such as the same outfit, a fixed view, or a bare silhouette, without rendering any pixels. Compared with explicit imagination in pixels, this latent imagination keeps nearly the same accuracy while being over 11x faster, at 5.9 ms per frame. Since each imagined condition has its own strengths, IDWorld then fuses them adaptively, weighting each condition for every probe-gallery pair. Extensive experiments show that IDWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on CCPG and CCGR-MINI, outperforms the compared methods on MEVID, and generalizes best to CASIA-B* and SUSTech1K, with the best mAP on CCVID. These results suggest identity world models offer a simple yet effective path to robust recognition. Code will be released.

197. 【2611.25179】Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179

作者：Zirui Song,Jiadong Zhang,Zheng Lu,Xiaosong Ma,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：measure policy performance, manipulation benchmarks measure, benchmarks measure policy, measure policy, policy performance

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Simulated manipulation benchmarks measure policy performance using hand-written success checkers that label each episode as a success or failure. Passing an expert demonstration shows that a checker accepts one way of completing the task. Learned policies, however, may stop after partial progress or complete the task in a different way. A checker may then accept an incomplete execution (phantom success) or reject an execution that satisfies the instruction (silent failure). In this paper, we introduce SimAudit, an LLM-based pipeline that audits success checkers against task instructions. SimAudit analyzes the instruction and checker code to identify scenarios where the two may disagree, recreates those scenarios in simulation, and runs the original checker. Multi-view renderings are then used to verify whether the observed behavior satisfies the instruction. Across RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa365, and RoboDojo, 183 simulated test cases produced the predicted checker verdict. Visual verification and deduplication yielded 61 distinct defects across 49 tasks, including 40 phantom successes and 21 silent failures. Most defects in RoboCasa365 accepted incomplete executions, most in RoboDojo rejected valid executions, and both error types were common in RoboTwin 2.0. These findings suggest that validating success checkers requires testing both incomplete executions and valid alternatives to expert demonstrations, and motivate more rigorous evaluation protocols for future benchmarks.

198. 【2611.25172】MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25172

作者：Yangchen Zhao,Ali Payani,Caiwen Ding

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：learn strong representations, encoders learn strong, learn strong, JEPA-based video encoders, video encoders learn

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:JEPA-based video encoders learn strong representations for video and image understanding through prediction in latent space. However, their standard pretraining predicts features within individual images or video clips, without explicitly exploiting paired camera views of the same object or moment. Consequently, the learned representations may not reliably support matching across different camera viewpoints. To solve this problem, we introduce multi-view predictive post-training to strengthen cross-view instance and temporal correspondence while retaining independent single-view inference. A shared encoder processes masked context views independently, and a training-time predictor jointly predicts latent tokens for masked context regions and a fully held-out view. We alternate cross-view prediction with single-view masked prediction on general image and video data to help retain the encoder's pretrained capabilities. After only 9,000 post-training updates on a single GPU, MV-JEPA improves cross-view instance retrieval and coarse cross-camera moment retrieval on various datasets, with further gains in zero-shot action-phase retrieval and frozen-feature classification on AE2. Frozen-probe evaluations further indicate that post-training largely preserves motion recognition, image recognition, and dense semantic segmentation performance on the evaluated benchmarks. Together, these results demonstrate that multi-view predictive post-training can strengthen instance and coarse temporal correspondence in JEPA video encoders without modifying the encoder architecture or single-view inference interface.

199. 【2611.25168】What a Calibrated Probability Hides: Belief-Level Post-Training for Decision Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25168

作者：Xinye Li,Zirui Song,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Zixiang Xu,Yinjun Liu,Zhewen Zhang,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：answer is chosen, BGPO, Policy, choice, calibrated decision model

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A calibrated decision model can predict how often each answer is chosen. The same prediction can reflect disagreement among people who are confident in their choices or uncertainty among people who share a belief. Disagreement calls for identifying whose perspective applies, while uncertainty calls for gathering more evidence. Standard post-training scores a single prediction against one observed choice at a time, learning the pooled distribution without distinguishing these two sources of uncertainty. We introduce Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization (BGPO). The policy proposes a list of beliefs, estimates their prevalence, and predicts a choice distribution for each. During training, BGPO uses Bayes' rule to estimate how likely each belief was to generate an observed choice. These estimates weight the choice's contribution to each belief's log-score reward. To discover rare beliefs, the policy also proposes lists after seeing a few observed choices and retains them only if they predict held-out choices well. At inference, the policy uses only the question and combines the belief-specific distributions by prevalence to produce a calibrated aggregate prediction. On benchmarks covering opinions, conversation safety, emotions elicited by artwork, and photo aesthetics, BGPO achieves the lowest conditional, worst-group, and overall Jensen-Shannon divergence across model initializations. It improves conditional divergence on both text and image tasks. On a synthetic image task with known beliefs, BGPO recovers the underlying groups more accurately than baselines trained with belief labels.

200. 【2611.25166】CoRelate: Coordinated Human-Object Motion Synthesis through Interaction Relations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25166

作者：Zhiwen Qiu,Daisy Lin,Diyu Zhou,Jialin Wu,Rosita Ji,Saleh Kalantari

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：synthesis requires coordinated, motion synthesis requires, requires coordinated human, Human-object motion synthesis, synthesis requires

备注： 11 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Human-object motion synthesis requires coordinated human participation as objects move toward specified goals. However, reaching object targets does not ensure that contact is established and maintained, while adjustments that improve contact may interfere with task completion. We introduce CoRelate, a diffusion framework that coordinates full-body human-object motion through explicit interaction relations and task feedback for rigid and articulated objects. We represent intended participation through hand-part relations that specify the participating hand, its part-relative reference position, and participation interval. These relations serve both as generation conditions and as references for measuring interaction realization. At each denoising step, a temporal refiner trained jointly with the generator combines relation deviations, task residuals, and motion features from the current prediction to jointly update human and object motion across the sequence. After generation, temporal motion correction addresses remaining contact failures by optimizing local motion segments while preserving object trajectories and protected human motion. Experiments demonstrate improved object-goal satisfaction, contact F1, and complete interaction success over the evaluated baselines. Ablation studies support the benefits of relation conditioning and the complementary roles of task and relation feedback.

201. 【2611.25134】GEMS: Generation and Experience Memory for Subgoal-Guided Long-Horizon World Model Planning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25134

作者：Yangchen Zhao,Ali Payani,Caiwen Ding

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：enable effective short-horizon, effective short-horizon goal-conditioned, Visual world models, models enable effective, reaching nearby targets

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual world models enable effective short-horizon goal-conditioned planning, such as reaching nearby targets and pushing objects over short distances, by predicting candidate action outcomes and comparing their representations with a goal representation. However, extending this approach to long-horizon tasks is challenging: prediction errors accumulate over extended rollouts, while latent-space similarity to distant goals provides limited guidance for ranking candidate outcomes. Existing methods address these limitations by introducing intermediate subgoals, either generated by learned models or selected from experience memory. Generated subgoals can contain hallucinations or be inaccurate in aspects such as object positions and timing. Memory-based approaches are constrained by experience coverage and may lack precise guidance for selecting appropriate intermediate states from stored observations. We introduce GEMS, which generates temporally structured subgoals to guide action search and state selection from Latent Experience Memory (LEM). A distance gate determines whether predicted outcomes are evaluated against the generated subgoal or the selected memory state. Experiments across six tasks and task suites spanning manipulation, navigation, and reaching demonstrate the effectiveness of GEMS for long-horizon planning. GEMS outperforms state-of-the-art world-model planning baselines across different manipulation settings, achieving success rates of 92.8% on Cube, 68.4% on Push-T, and 54.1% on Meta-World. It improves over the strongest evaluated baseline on these tasks by 24.0, 7.3, and 22.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate that generated subgoals and experience memory can jointly strengthen long-horizon planning with a frozen world model.

202. 【2611.25126】ALD: Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement for Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25126

作者：Hui Li,Pengfei Yang,Xinyi Feng,Junhong Liu,Tianyang Zheng,Hui Zeng,Le Dong,Kaibing Zhang,Mengting Zhao,Quan Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：modality discriminative characteristics, source-modality supervision conflicts, target modality discriminative, incur negative transfer, discriminative characteristics

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-modal knowledge distillation can incur negative transfer when source-modality supervision conflicts with the target modality's discriminative characteristics. We propose TALD, a two-stage framework for Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement in class-aligned logit space. Guided by task-dependent self-benefiting and cross-modal exclusivity criteria, a Residual Disentangler decomposes logits from a teacher and an independently trained target-modality reference student into modality-general and modality-specific components. A Sample-Adaptive Modulator regulates decomposition constraints and sample-specific fusion weights. The learned modality-general components are then adaptively fused to supervise the final student. Across five benchmarks spanning vision, audio, and text, TALD achieves the largest mean accuracy gain and highest positive-transfer frequency among the compared methods. Across ten transfer directions with five runs each, TALD improves over training without distillation in 84% of evaluations, compared with at most 70% for the baselines, while offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We plan to release the code.

203. 【2611.25113】Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113

作者：Linbo Tang,Aneel Damaraju,Todd Zickler

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：embodied visual systems, Localizing object boundaries, Localizing object, visual systems, physical surfaces

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Localizing object boundaries is important for embodied visual systems, since small boundary errors can change the inferred geometry of physical surfaces. We study whether deep stereo models produce boundaries consistent with simple shape priors when local image and disparity cues are weak. We create a synthetic stereo dataset consisting of objects drawn from a highly constrained family of shapes, with randomized depth, slant, color and texture. Despite the simplicity of this shape space, we find that several state-of-the-art stereo models retain systematic boundary errors, even when finetuned or trained from scratch on the dataset. We then introduce a simple generative stereo model that jointly predicts disparity and a distance field representing object boundaries. The model improves boundary localization and produces contours that adhere more closely to the underlying shape space. These results expose limitations in the boundary predictions of current stereo architectures when local evidence is weak, and motivate further study of whether generative models can better combine regional grouping evidence with global geometric structure.

204. 【2611.25108】Long-Range Video Presentation Attack Detection: A Three-Class Benchmark Across 1-100 m and Two Sensor Types

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25108

作者：Lambert Igene,Mohammad Zahir Uddin Chowdhury,Afzal Hossain,Sandip Purnapatra,Peter Johnson,Stephanie Schuckers

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Face presentation attack, presentation attack detection, Face presentation, subjects are captured, close range

备注： 10 page, IJCB

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Face presentation attack detection (PAD) systems deployed in border control and airport surveillance are constrained by a critical assumption: subjects are captured at close range, typically under one meter. No existing benchmark systematically addresses multi-distance capture, diverse environmental conditions, and concurrent spoofing and occlusion-based attacks, leaving deployed models underprepared for real-world scenarios. We introduce a video-based PAD dataset of 374,000+ frames from 261 unique identities collected across five standoff distances (1 m-100 m) under indoor and outdoor conditions using a DSLR camera (C) and a mobile sensor (M), with three-class labels: live, spoof, and props (occlusions); models are trained and evaluated on video clips rather than single frames. We fine-tune and benchmark four CNN architectures: MobileNetV3, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and ResNet-50. The higher-resolution DSLR maintains near-perfect spoof classification rate across all distances, while the lower-resolution mobile sensor degrades sharply beyond 50 m, with props classification rate dropping to 55% at 75 m for MobileNetV3. EfficientNet achieves the best overall performance (98.48% accuracy, ACER 2.01%). Generalization tests on unseen attack datasets yield 89-94% accuracy, validating cross-domain robustness. These results establish capture distance and sensor resolution as critical but underexplored axes of PAD difficulty. The dataset will be publicly released under an End-User License Agreement to support reproducible long-range PAD research.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25105

作者：Xianzhe Fan,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Jing Wang,Yecheng Wu,Yu-Cheng Chou,Johan Bjorck,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Jan Kautz,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Zhiding Yu,Hengshuang Zhao,Zhiqi Li

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Every move in a robot team changes the world in which its teammates must act. Coordinated manipulation therefore calls for predicting the team's actions together with the physical future they jointly create. We introduce MAWAM, a multi-agent world-action model that jointly generates each robot's future views and executable action chunks in a single sample. Built from a pretrained single-robot world-action backbone, MAWAM jointly denoises all robots' action and future-view tokens through cross-agent attention, while preserving a separate control interface for each robot. MAWAM achieves the highest success on all three real-robot tasks (laundry-bag collection, bath-towel folding, and umbrella handover), with 67.8% mean success, 18.9 percentage points above MA-VLA trained on the same demonstrations and cameras. In simulation, MAWAM reaches 90.3% mean success on eight tasks with teams of up to four arms. Ablations show that cross-agent attention and team-stage future-video supervision both improve coordinated control: without attention, a four-arm relay falls from 95.0% to 39.0%, and without this supervision, Place Food falls from 78.0% to 47.0%.
