链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25069

作者：Haoran Xu,Tian Chen,Yuhe Wu,Yanbo Zhao,Junchi Liao,Hongyu Wang,Jiaze Li,Wentao Wang,Yanfeng Li,Rui Qian

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：multimodal large language, On-policy self-distillation, improving visual reasoning, large language models, offers a promising

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising approach to improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, not all samples benefit equally from privileged visual guidance, and uniform token supervision can dilute visually grounded signals. We propose VisAlloc, a dual-level framework combining sample-level visual filtering with token-level counterfactual reweighting. A fixed paired-teacher filter retains samples answered correctly from an evidence-centered positive crop but incorrectly from a spatially displaced negative crop. At the token level, both EMA teachers score the same student-generated response, and their absolute log-probability differences measure view sensitivity. Additional supervision targets tokens admitted by sensitivity or teacher-distribution disagreement, with shared frozen-prior, template-bigram, and student--teacher divergence gates. Inverse-frequency redistribution preserves the total additional weight within each micro-batch, while the positive teacher remains the sole distillation target. Inference requires only the full-image student. Across eight benchmarks, our 4B and 9B models achieve macro-averages of 81.44 and 84.92, outperforming Vision-OPD reproductions by 0.97 and 0.52 points, respectively. Code on GitHub: this https URL

207. 【2611.25061】Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25061

作者：Amani Kiruga,Mark Hamilton,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,William T. Freeman,Vincent Sitzmann,Yilun Du,Ayush Tewari

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：extreme viewpoints, highly unfamiliar, unfamiliar or extreme, generative, Abstract

备注： 23 pages, 12 figures, 15 tables. Includes references and appendices. Project page: [this https URL](https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Humans can understand the 3D structure of scenes even from highly unfamiliar or extreme viewpoints. Cognitive science suggests this generalization stems from a top-down generative inference strategy. In contrast, state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction neural networks typically rely on amortized, feedforward inference. While fast, this pattern-matching approach degrades when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) scene conditions unseen during training. Inspired by the generative nature of human vision, we propose Infer3D, an inverse generative modeling framework that recovers the latent variables of a generative model to best match the monocular input. To prevent degenerate flat outputs, we couple the generative image prior with a frozen 3D lifting network that constrains the optimization space to a valid, multi-view-consistent 3D manifold. Across a variety of controlled synthetic and real-world tasks, Infer3D demonstrates superior OOD reconstruction accuracy compared to feedforward networks trained on equal or larger datasets. Furthermore, mirroring how human perception requires more time to process confusing stimuli, we introduce an adaptive inference strategy that expends more test-time compute on ambiguous OOD examples while efficiently routing in-distribution inputs. Project website: this https URL

208. 【2611.25005】Inhabitant: An Agent Framework for Long-Horizon Human-Scene Interaction with Spatial Control and Quantitative Feedback

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25005

作者：Yao Zhang,Zhuchenyang Liu,Yu Xiao

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recent work, language-model agent plans, generation moves, work on human-scene, moves from single

备注： [this https URL](https://ryenhails.github.io/Inhabitant/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent work on human-scene interaction (HSI) generation moves from single interactions to long-horizon interaction, where a language-model agent plans and executes a sequence of interactions in a 3D scene under one instruction. However, existing agents struggle in challenging everyday scenarios (e.g., picking up a toy and throwing it into a basket) because they lack spatial control over the motion and quantitative feedback on its execution, and they often fail to complete the interaction sequence. To address these gaps, we present Inhabitant, a training-free agent framework in which the agent specifies each motion through positions, paths and poses, measures how the executed interaction deviates from its intent, and revises the control specification accordingly. For an extensive evaluation in such scenarios, we build Inhabit, a benchmark of 75 household tasks in 31 furniture layouts of an apartment, in which every task chains one to three goals on movable objects and is carried out by the whole body, excluding fingers. On Inhabit, Inhabitant completes 98.7\% of the tasks with Claude Opus 5.5, and 93.3\% and 82.7\% with Claude Sonnet 5.5 and Gemini 4 Argon, respectively. Ablations confirm that without spatial control the agent fails most tasks, and without quantitative feedback its motion becomes less precise. Code is available on the project page.

209. 【2611.25001】Density Feedback Control for Transferability of Generative Sparse Adversarial Attacks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25001

作者：Dongsu Song,Jay Hoon Jung

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Generator-based sparse adversarial, produce transferable sparse, black-box robustness evaluation, Generator-based sparse, transferable sparse perturbations

备注： preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generator-based sparse adversarial attacks train encoder--decoder networks to produce transferable sparse perturbations for black-box robustness evaluation. Their perturbation density, however, is set only indirectly by a fixed sparsity coefficient whose mapping to density is unknown and shifts during training, so reaching a target density requires re-tuning; this fixed pressure also yields little transferability gain once density reduction begins. We propose Density Feedback Control (DFC), which tightens or relaxes this coefficient at the end of each epoch according to the gap between the observed hard-mask density and the target, without changing the generator architecture or loss. At a 10\% target density, DFC brings the mean density of three base attacks within one percentage point of the target in a single run and improves the transfer attack success rate (ASR) of two of them on CNN and Transformer targets, including under per-image $\ell_0$ budgets, while perturbing fewer pixels. Tracing GPG's training, we observe that as DFC relaxes the coefficient, the transfer ASR recovers with the density and overtakes the fixed-coefficient baseline with fewer pixels. For EGS-TSSA, which restricts perturbations to source-model feature-importance regions, DFC yields no consistent gain; we analyze how this restriction interacts with density feedback.

210. 【2611.24995】EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995

作者：Hongyu Wang,Chuhan Li,Congjin Xu,Xuandong Zhao,Hua Shen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：environment requires reasoning, egocentric environment requires, surrounding entities affect, environment requires, requires reasoning

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer's actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

211. 【2611.24960】ETHER: Training-Free, Elastic, ToC-Anchored Hierarchy Extraction and Recovery in Design-Driven Long Documents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24960

作者：Yu Zou,Yan Chen,Jialei Zeng,Xiaorui Zhou,Aixi Zhong,Qi Zhou,Haoran You,Ke Jiang,Changyu Hu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：deploying Large Language, Large Language Models, Converting complex documents, structured knowledge constitutes, critical infrastructure urgently

备注： EMNLP 2026 Main

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Converting complex documents into structured knowledge constitutes the critical infrastructure urgently required for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in downstream research. However, blind to the Table of Contents (ToC) as a macro-structure, existing bottom-up paradigms rely on localized visual cues, inducing heuristic fragility in high-entropy layouts where visual prominence decouples from logical depth; meanwhile, direct LLM extraction lacks spatial grounding, triggering hallucinations in long contexts. To address this, we introduce TETHER, a training-free framework that elevates the ToC from discarded noise to a vital structural prior, enabling robust adaptation across design-driven long documents. TETHER operates via a two-stage neuro-symbolic mechanism: first, it constructs a document skeleton from the raw ToC using a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) empowered by spatio-semantic constraints; second, it employs an anchor-based alignment strategy to tether content blocks to this skeleton, effectively resolving boundary ambiguities. Evaluated across a multilingual benchmark of ESG reports, product manuals, and annual reports, TETHER delivers highly precise, state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot hierarchy recovery, outperforming deep hierarchical recovery models, dedicated parsers, and general-purpose LMMs. Moreover, stress tests highlight TETHER's resilience to sparse omissions and textual noise, demonstrating that its precise semantic alignment is fundamentally anchored in topological and depth fidelity. Crucially, ablations confirm the framework's architectural irreducibility, while achieving 90% token reduction and drastic speedups over end-to-end baselines, establishing a compelling equilibrium between structural precision and deployment scalability.

212. 【2611.25510】Life-IQA+: Boosting Blind Image Quality Assessment via GCN-enhanced Layer Interaction and Flexible Hierarchical Expert Specialization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25510

作者：Long Tang,Guoquan Zheng,Huiyu Duan,Jie Hao,Yucheng Zhu,Liang Yuan,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Blind image quality, optimizing visual experience, Blind image, image quality assessment, Existing BIQA methods

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) is essential for evaluating and optimizing visual experience. Existing BIQA methods typically aggregate features from multiple backbone stages without accounting for their unequal contributions to quality prediction. Furthermore, uniformly supervising all experts may constrain their flexibility and limit the diversity of the learned quality representations. To address these issues, we present Life-IQA+, a quality representation decoding framework that combines graph convolutional network (GCN) enhanced layer interaction and flexible hierarchical expert specialization. Specifically, the layer interaction module constructs quality queries from GCN-refined deepest-stage features and uses penultimate-stage features as keys and values, thereby enabling cross-layer representation interaction via the cross-attention mechanism. Building on these representations, we further introduce a flexible hierarchical mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture comprising a densely activated shared expert layer and a sparsely activated routed expert layer. To enhance the representation diversity and ability of experts, we apply auxiliary classification supervision to a subset of shared experts using distortion-type labels for synthetic datasets and discretized quality-level labels for authentic datasets. Experiments on synthetic and authentic distortion benchmarks, together with controlled ablations of expert configurations and supervision strategies, show that Life-IQA+ achieves competitive prediction accuracy with a favorable balance between accuracy and computational cost. The code is available at: this https URL.

213. 【2611.25335】Breaking Noise Shortcuts in Self-Supervised Learning Via Noise-Aligned View Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25335

作者：Oankar R. Patil,May D. Wang

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Self-supervised learning, SEANA, increasingly applied, applied to scientific, labels are scarce

备注： 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-supervised learning (SSL) is increasingly applied to scientific imaging domains, such as microscopy, where labels are scarce but noisy data is abundant. These domains exhibit significant signal-dependent noise, which can create a representational shortcut: since both augmented views derive from the same noisy image, the encoder can boost view agreement by also encoding noise patterns rather than semantic content. To enable noise-robust representation learning without knowledge of underlying noise models, we introduce Self-Aligned Noise Augmentation (SEANA), a drop-in module that generates noise-aware SSL views. SEANA learns an invertible variance-stabilizing transform (VST) from noisy data, then estimates the clean signal and resamples independent noise in learned VST space, requiring no clean targets, no dataset-specific denoiser training or use at inference, and no changes to the SSL objective or encoder. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 under synthetic Gaussian, Poisson-Gaussian, and multiplicative noise, SEANA improves clean-test linear-probe accuracy across contrastive/non-contrastive SSL methods, indicating higher-quality representations. On real fluorescence imaging, SEANA improves Jurkat cell-cycle stage classification by up to 28.6pp over standard SSL and 27.5pp over denoiser-preprocessed baselines. In all settings, SEANA outperforms the denoiser-preprocessed SSL pipelines, demonstrating that learned VST-space noise-aligned resampling yields more robust representations than denoising alone. Code is available at: this https URL
